<script id="ethvelle-v125-media-volume-timeline-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const safeTransform=t=>({scale:Number(t&&t.scale!=null?t.scale:1)||1,offsetX:Number(t&&t.offsetX)||0,offsetY:Number(t&&t.offsetY)||0,rotation:Number(t&&t.rotation)||0});
  const selectedOverlay=()=>{
    try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip))||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  };

  /* VOLUME — continuous CapCut-style range with labelled stops, not preset cards. */
  window.volumeTargetClip=function(){
    if(typeof selectedClip!=='string'||!Array.isArray(overlays))return null;
    const clip=overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip));
    return clip&&(clip.type==='video'||clip.type==='audio')?clip:null;
  };
  function volumePanelPaint(clip,pct){
    const slider=$('evvVolumeSlider');
    if(slider){slider.value=String(pct);slider.style.setProperty('--evv-volume-fill',(pct/2)+'%');}
    const value=$('volumeVal');if(value)value.textContent=Math.round(pct)+'%';
    const header=$('volumePanelHeader');if(header)header.textContent=(clip.name||'Clip')+' · '+Math.round(pct)+'%';
    document.querySelectorAll('#volumePanelBody .evv-volume-marks span').forEach((el)=>{
      const v=Number(el.dataset.stop)||0;el.style.color=Math.abs(v-pct)<1?'#AFC4FF':'#8B91A2';
    });
  }
  window.renderVolumePanel=function(){
    const body=$('volumePanelBody');if(!body)return;
    const clip=window.volumeTargetClip();
    if(!clip){body.innerHTML='<p class="field-label">Select a video or audio clip on the timeline to change its volume.</p>';return;}
    const pct=clamp(Math.round((clip.volume==null?1:Number(clip.volume))*100),0,200);
    const marks=[0,50,100,150,200];
    const isVideo=clip.type==='video';
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="evv-volume-panel">
      <div class="evv-volume-head"><span class="evv-volume-name" id="volumePanelHeader">${typeof escapeHtml==='function'?escapeHtml(clip.name||'Clip'):(clip.name||'Clip')}</span><output class="evv-volume-value" id="volumeVal">${pct}%</output></div>
      <div class="evv-volume-slider-wrap">
        <input id="evvVolumeSlider" class="evv-volume-slider" type="range" min="0" max="200" step="1" value="${pct}" style="--evv-volume-fill:${pct/2}%" oninput="window.volumePanelSetVolume(this.value)" aria-label="Clip volume">
        <div class="evv-volume-stop-points" aria-hidden="true">${marks.map(()=>'<i></i>').join('')}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="evv-volume-marks">${marks.map(v=>`<span data-stop="${v}">${v}%</span>`).join('')}</div>
      ${isVideo?'<button type="button" class="evv-volume-extract" onclick="window.extractAudioFromClip()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3v12m0 0 4-4m-4 4-4-4M4 17v3h16v-3"/></svg><span>Extract audio to its own clip</span></button>':''}
      <button type="button" class="evv-volume-done" onclick="closePanel()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    volumePanelPaint(clip,pct);
  };
  window.volumePanelSetVolume=function(value){
    const clip=window.volumeTargetClip();if(!clip)return;
    const pct=clamp(value,0,200),volume=pct/100;
    const wasExtractMuted=!!clip.__ethvelleExtractedAudioMuted;
    clip.volume=volume;
    if(wasExtractMuted&&pct>0){
      clip.__ethvelleExtractedAudioMuted=false;
      if(Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes))clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.forEach(k=>{if(k&&k.property==='volume')k.value=volume;});
      if(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase,'volume'))clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.volume=volume;
    }
    if(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase,'volume'))clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.volume=volume;
    const chosen=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
    if(chosen&&String(chosen.id)===String(clip.id)&&Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)){
      const k=clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes[Number(chosen.index)];if(k&&k.property==='volume')k.value=volume;
    }
    if(clip.type==='audio'&&typeof audioOverlayNodes!=='undefined'&&audioOverlayNodes[clip.id]&&typeof audioOverlayAudioCtx!=='undefined'&&audioOverlayAudioCtx){
      try{audioOverlayNodes[clip.id].gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(volume,audioOverlayAudioCtx.currentTime,0.015);}catch(_e){try{audioOverlayNodes[clip.id].gain.gain.value=volume;}catch(__e){}}
    }
    const video=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[clip.id]:null;
    if(video&&clip.type==='video'){try{video.muted=false;video.volume=Math.min(1,volume);}catch(_e){}}
    volumePanelPaint(clip,pct);
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(getCurrentAbsoluteTime());}catch(_e){}
    if(typeof debouncedPersist==='function')debouncedPersist();else if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
    if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
  };

  /* Extract the same source at the same timeline position, while retaining durable IndexedDB refs. */
  window.extractAudioFromClip=function(){
    const clip=window.volumeTargetClip();
    if(!clip||clip.type!=='video'){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a video clip to extract its audio',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    let id='ov'+Date.now()+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6);
    while(overlays.some(o=>String(o.id)===String(id)))id='ov'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9);
    const newClip={
      id,name:(clip.name||'Video')+' (audio)',color:clip.color,type:'audio',src:clip.src,
      deviceMediaKey:clip.deviceMediaKey||undefined,
      deviceMediaName:clip.deviceMediaName||clip.name||undefined,
      deviceMediaSize:Number(clip.deviceMediaSize)||undefined,
      deviceMediaLastModified:Number(clip.deviceMediaLastModified)||undefined,
      sourceDuration:Number(clip.sourceDuration)||Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(clip.trimEnd)-Number(clip.trimStart)),
      sourceOffset:Math.max(0,Number(clip.sourceOffset)||0),
      trimStart:Number(clip.trimStart)||0,trimEnd:Number(clip.trimEnd)||MIN_CLIP_SEC,
      thumbs:[],peaks:Array.isArray(clip.peaks)&&clip.peaks.length?clip.peaks.slice():null,
      speed:Number(clip.speed)||1,volume:1,transform:null,
      audioSettings:clip.audioSettings&&typeof clip.audioSettings==='object'?{...clip.audioSettings}:undefined
    };
    overlays.push(newClip);
    timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,newClip.trimEnd);
    /* Mute the original independently; the extracted audio remains at 100%. */
    clip.volume=0;clip.__ethvelleExtractedAudioMuted=true;
    if(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase,'volume'))clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.volume=0;
    if(Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes))clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.forEach(k=>{if(k&&k.property==='volume')k.value=0;});
    const originalEl=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[clip.id]:null;
    if(originalEl){try{originalEl.muted=false;originalEl.volume=0;}catch(_e){}}
    if(!newClip.peaks&&typeof generateAudioWaveform==='function'){
      const waveformSource=newClip.src;
      generateAudioWaveform(waveformSource).then(result=>{
        if(!overlays.includes(newClip))return;
        newClip.peaks=Array.from(result&&result.peaks||[]);
        if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();
        if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
      }).catch(err=>console.warn('Ethvelle: extracted audio waveform could not be generated',err));
    }
    renderOverlayLayers();syncTimelineInnerWidth();renderRuler();
    selectClipForChrome(newClip.id);renderOverlayListPanel();renderFrame();
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Audio extracted · original video muted',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1800});
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    if(typeof renderVolumePanel==='function')renderVolumePanel();
  };

  /* A muted clip is still a video/audio clip: keep Volume available and refresh its label. */
  const priorBottomSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorBottomSync==='function'&&!priorBottomSync.__evv125Volume){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=priorBottomSync.apply(this,arguments);try{
      const clip=window.volumeTargetClip(),tool=$('clipVolumeTool'),label=$('clipVolumeLabel');
      const keyframeMode=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null;
      if(tool&&!keyframeMode)tool.style.display=clip?'flex':'none';
      if(label&&clip){const p=Math.round((clip.volume==null?1:Number(clip.volume))*100);label.textContent=p===100?'Volume':(p===0?'Muted':p+'%');}
    }catch(_e){}return out;};
    wrapped.__evv125Volume=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const priorPanelOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorPanelOpen==='function'&&!priorPanelOpen.__evv125Volume){
    const wrapped=function(key){const out=priorPanelOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='volume')requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.renderVolumePanel());return out;};
    wrapped.__evv125Volume=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }



  /* Clip taps are selection-only. The timeline's own gesture handlers still scrub on a
     deliberate horizontal drag, but a stationary tap never changes time or scroll position. */

  /* Keep selected media transforms and their rendered picture/keyframe data in sync. */
  function getLiveVisualTransform(clip,time){
    try{const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',time):null;if(keyed)return safeTransform(keyed);}catch(_e){}
    return safeTransform(clip&&clip.transform);
  }
  function shiftTransformValue(value,dx,dy,dr,scaleRatio){
    const t=safeTransform(value);return {scale:Math.max(.001,t.scale*scaleRatio),offsetX:t.offsetX+dx,offsetY:t.offsetY+dy,rotation:t.rotation+dr};
  }
  function commitVisualTransform(clip,next,previous,time){
    if(!clip)return;
    next=safeTransform(next);previous=safeTransform(previous||clip.transform);
    const keys=Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
    const transformKeys=keys.filter(k=>k&&k.property==='transform');
    const tolerance=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow():.05;
    let targetKey=null;
    const selected=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
    if(selected&&String(selected.id)===String(clip.id)){
      const k=keys[Number(selected.index)];if(k&&k.property==='transform')targetKey=k;
    }
    if(!targetKey&&transformKeys.length){
      targetKey=transformKeys.find(k=>Math.abs(Number(k.t)-Number(time))<=tolerance)||null;
    }
    if(targetKey){targetKey.value={...next};}
    else if(transformKeys.length){
      const dx=next.offsetX-previous.offsetX,dy=next.offsetY-previous.offsetY,dr=next.rotation-previous.rotation,ratio=previous.scale?next.scale/previous.scale:1;
      transformKeys.forEach(k=>{k.value=shiftTransformValue(k.value,dx,dy,dr,ratio);});
      if(clip.transform)clip.transform=shiftTransformValue(clip.transform,dx,dy,dr,ratio);
      if(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase&&clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform)clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform=shiftTransformValue(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform,dx,dy,dr,ratio);
    }else{
      clip.transform={...next};
      if(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase,'transform'))clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframeBase.transform={...next};
    }
  }
  const priorTransformUpdate=window.updateTransform;
  if(typeof priorTransformUpdate==='function'&&!priorTransformUpdate.__evv125Sync){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const clip=selectedOverlay();
      if(!clip||(clip.type!=='video'&&clip.type!=='image'))return priorTransformUpdate.apply(this,arguments);
      const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      const before=getLiveVisualTransform(clip,now);
      const size=$('sizeSlider'),x=$('posXSlider'),y=$('posYSlider'),rot=$('rotSlider');
      if(!size||!x||!y||!rot)return priorTransformUpdate.apply(this,arguments);
      const next={scale:Number(size.value)/100,offsetX:(Number(x.value)/100)*(stageW*.75),offsetY:(Number(y.value)/100)*(stageH*.75),rotation:Number(rot.value)||0};
      commitVisualTransform(clip,next,before,now);
      $('sizeVal').textContent=Number(size.value)+'%';$('posXVal').textContent=String(x.value);$('posYVal').textContent=String(y.value);$('rotVal').textContent=String(rot.value)+'°';
      renderFrame();if(typeof debouncedPersist==='function')debouncedPersist();else if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
    };
    wrapped.__evv125Sync=true;window.updateTransform=wrapped;
  }
  const priorTransformSync=window.syncTransformSliders;
  if(typeof priorTransformSync==='function'&&!priorTransformSync.__evv125Sync){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=priorTransformSync.apply(this,arguments);try{
      const clip=selectedOverlay();if(clip&&(clip.type==='video'||clip.type==='image')){
        const t=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        const live=getLiveVisualTransform(clip,t),size=$('sizeSlider'),x=$('posXSlider'),y=$('posYSlider'),rot=$('rotSlider');
        if(size)size.value=Math.round(live.scale*100);
        if(x)x.value=Math.round(live.offsetX/Math.max(1,stageW*.75));
        if(y)y.value=Math.round(live.offsetY/Math.max(1,stageH*.75));
        if(rot)rot.value=Math.round(live.rotation);
        if($('sizeVal'))$('sizeVal').textContent=size.value+'%';if($('posXVal'))$('posXVal').textContent=x.value;if($('posYVal'))$('posYVal').textContent=y.value;if($('rotVal'))$('rotVal').textContent=rot.value+'°';
      }
    }catch(_e){}return result;};
    wrapped.__evv125Sync=true;window.syncTransformSliders=wrapped;
  }
  const priorFinishSel=window.finishSelBoxEdit;
  if(typeof priorFinishSel==='function'&&!priorFinishSel.__evv125Sync){
    const wrapped=function(tg){const result=priorFinishSel.apply(this,arguments);try{
      if(tg&&tg.kind==='overlay'){
        const clip=selectedOverlay();if(clip&&(clip.type==='video'||clip.type==='image')){
          const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
          const current=getLiveVisualTransform(clip,now);commitVisualTransform(clip,tg.obj,current,now);
          if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();
          if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
        }
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v125 transform commit:',err);}return result;};
    wrapped.__evv125Sync=true;window.finishSelBoxEdit=wrapped;
  }

  /* Re-assert video frame readiness after each overlay render; no per-frame DOM rebuilding. */
  const priorOverlayRender=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof priorOverlayRender==='function'&&!priorOverlayRender.__evv125Video){
    const wrapped=function(time){const result=priorOverlayRender.apply(this,arguments);try{
      const now=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():0);
      (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
        if(!ov||ov.type!=='video')return;
        const el=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;if(!el)return;
        const active=now>=Number(ov.trimStart)&&now<Number(ov.trimEnd);
        if(!active){if(!el.paused)try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}return;}
        el.playsInline=true;el.setAttribute('playsinline','');el.preload='auto';
        if(el.dataset.evv125ReadyHook!=='1'){
          el.dataset.evv125ReadyHook='1';
          el.addEventListener('loadeddata',()=>{
            const t=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():0;
            if(t>=Number(ov.trimStart)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd)){
              try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
            }
          },{once:true});
          el.addEventListener('error',()=>console.warn('Ethvelle: video clip failed to decode',ov.name||ov.id),{once:true});
        }
      });
    }catch(_e){}return result;};
    wrapped.__evv125Video=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }

  /* Credits: preserve the final split capsule during auth/Firestore loading; only the value is pending. */
  window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading=!!currentUser;
  window.renderHomeCreditSummary=function(){
    const planEl=$('homeAccountPlan'),balanceEl=$('homeAccountBalance'),button=$('homeAccountSummary'),profileButton=$('homeProfileBtn');
    if(profileButton)profileButton.style.display=currentUser?'flex':'none';if(!planEl||!balanceEl||!button)return;
    if(!currentUser){planEl.textContent='Login';balanceEl.textContent='Sign in to view credits';button.setAttribute('aria-label','Login to view your plan and credits');if(typeof renderHomeProfileAvatar==='function')renderHomeProfileAvatar();return;}
    const owned=typeof getMyOwnedPlansForBilling==='function'?getMyOwnedPlansForBilling():[];
    const names=[...new Set((owned||[]).map(p=>String(p&&p.name||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
    const planLabel=(names.length?names.join(' + '):getMyActivePlanName())+' plan';
    const monthly=typeof getMyCombinedCreditAllowance==='function'?Math.max(0,Number(getMyCombinedCreditAllowance(owned))||0):0;
    const available=Math.max(0,Number(myCreditsBalance)||0);
    planEl.textContent=planLabel;
    const availableText=window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading?'...':available.toLocaleString();
    const monthlyText=window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading?'...':monthly.toLocaleString();
    balanceEl.innerHTML=`<span class="home-credit-available">${availableText}</span><span class="home-credit-divider">/</span><span class="home-credit-monthly">${monthlyText}</span><span class="home-credit-unit">credits</span>`;
    button.setAttribute('aria-label',planLabel+', '+(window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading?'loading credits':available.toLocaleString()+' credits available out of '+monthly.toLocaleString()+' monthly credits')+'. Open billing and plan');
    if(typeof renderHomeProfileAvatar==='function')renderHomeProfileAvatar();
  };
  /* Replace only this listener setup so loading ends on the actual first Firestore snapshot. */
  window.startMyCreditsSync=function(uid){
    window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading=!!(uid&&firebaseReady&&fbDb);
    try{if(typeof stopMyCreditsSync==='function')stopMyCreditsSync();}catch(_e){}
    if(!(firebaseReady&&fbDb)||!uid){window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading=false;if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();return;}
    if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();
    try{
      myCreditsUserUnsub=fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).onSnapshot(doc=>{
        const profile=doc.exists?(doc.data()||{}):{};
        if(typeof ethvelleIsAdminBanActive==='function'&&ethvelleIsAdminBanActive(profile)&&ethvelleBanSignoutUid!==String(uid)){
          ethvelleBanSignoutUid=String(uid);showToast(profile.adminBanReason?'Account access restricted: '+String(profile.adminBanReason).slice(0,100):'This account has been suspended',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3000});
          if(fbAuth&&fbAuth.currentUser&&fbAuth.currentUser.uid===uid)fbAuth.signOut().catch(()=>{});return;
        }
        if(typeof ethvelleIsAdminBanActive==='function'&&!ethvelleIsAdminBanActive(profile)&&ethvelleBanSignoutUid===String(uid))ethvelleBanSignoutUid='';
        myCreditsBalance=doc.exists?Math.max(0,Number(profile.creditsBalance)||0):0;
        mergeHiddenCreditIds(uid,profile.hiddenCreditTransactionIds);
        window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading=false;
        if(typeof renderMyPlanScreen==='function')renderMyPlanScreen();
        if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();
        if(typeof renderExportCreditEstimate==='function')renderExportCreditEstimate();
      },err=>{console.warn('Ethvelle: credit balance sync failed',err);window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading=false;if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();});
      myCreditTransactionsUnsub=fbDb.collection('creditTransactions').where('uid','==',uid).limit(100).onSnapshot(snap=>{
        myCreditTransactions=snap.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()})).sort((a,b)=>creditTimestampMs(b.createdAt)-creditTimestampMs(a.createdAt));
        if(typeof renderMyPlanScreen==='function')renderMyPlanScreen();
        if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();
      },err=>console.warn('Ethvelle: credit history sync failed',err));
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: could not start credit sync',err);window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading=false;if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();}
  };

  /* Repaint homepage logo on decode without changing its aspect ratio or clipping its edges. */
  const homeLogo=$('screen-home')&&$('screen-home').querySelector('.home-brand-logo-only');
  if(homeLogo){homeLogo.decoding='async';homeLogo.loading='eager';homeLogo.addEventListener('load',()=>{homeLogo.style.opacity='1';},{once:true});homeLogo.addEventListener('error',()=>{homeLogo.style.opacity='1';},{once:true});}
  try{if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(_e){}
  try{if(currentUser&&currentUser.uid)window.startMyCreditsSync(currentUser.uid);else if(typeof renderHomeCreditSummary==='function')renderHomeCreditSummary();}catch(_e){}
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v126-timeline-polish-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const finite=(v,fallback=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):fallback;
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,finite(v,a)));

  /* Re-render the same slider without any decorative stop dots. */
  const originalVolumeRender=window.renderVolumePanel;
  if(typeof originalVolumeRender==='function'&&!originalVolumeRender.__evv126NoDots){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=originalVolumeRender.apply(this,arguments);
      const stops=$('#volumePanelBody .evv-volume-stop-points');
      if(stops)stops.remove();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv126NoDots=true;window.renderVolumePanel=wrapped;
  }

  /* Cross-row clip taps use the same selection-only policy as taps within a row.
     New-clip insertion is handled centrally by onClipSelected(clip,'add'). */

  /* Resolve a tapped diamond by its on-screen time, not only a possibly stale array index.
     The key object is retained through selection so sorting/re-rendering cannot select its
     former neighbour after a keyframe is moved or another key is inserted. */
  function nearestKeyByMarkerPosition(clip,keys,marker){
    if(!clip||!Array.isArray(keys)||!keys.length||!marker)return null;
    const x=parseFloat(marker.style.left),pps=Math.max(1,finite(typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?pxPerSecond():100,100));
    if(!Number.isFinite(x))return null;
    const target=(finite(clip.trimStart)+x/pps);
    let best=null,distance=Infinity;
    keys.forEach(key=>{const d=Math.abs(finite(key&&key.t,Infinity)-target);if(d<distance){distance=d;best=key;}});
    return best&&distance<=Math.max(.16,14/pps)?best:null;
  }
  let pendingKeyTap=null;
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#screen-editor #timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(!marker||e.isPrimary===false)return;
    const objectTrack=marker.closest('.object-keyframe-track'),rawObject=marker.getAttribute('data-object-kf-idx');
    if(objectTrack&&rawObject!=null){
      const id=String(objectTrack.dataset.objectKfTrack||'');
      const clip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
      const key=nearestKeyByMarkerPosition(clip,keys,marker)||keys[Number(rawObject)]||null;
      pendingKeyTap={kind:'object',id,index:Number(rawObject),key,at:Date.now(),pointerId:e.pointerId};
      return;
    }
    const rawChar=marker.getAttribute('data-kf-idx');
    if(rawChar!=null){
      const clipEl=marker.closest('.anim-clip');
      const ref=clipEl&&clipEl.id==='animClipEl'?'main':clipEl&&clipEl.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+clipEl.dataset.segIdx:'';
      const clip=ref==='main'?activeClip:(ref&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(ref):null);
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)?clip.keyframes:[];
      const key=nearestKeyByMarkerPosition(clip,keys,marker)||keys[Number(rawChar)]||null;
      pendingKeyTap={kind:'character',id:ref,ref,index:Number(rawChar),key,at:Date.now(),pointerId:e.pointerId};
    }
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(e){if(pendingKeyTap&&pendingKeyTap.pointerId===e.pointerId)pendingKeyTap=null;},true);

  const priorSelectObjectKeyframe=window.selectObjectKeyframe;
  if(typeof priorSelectObjectKeyframe==='function'&&!priorSelectObjectKeyframe.__evv126Stable){
    const wrapped=function(id,index){
      const clip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?clip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
      const pending=pendingKeyTap;pendingKeyTap=null;
      const stable=(pending&&pending.kind==='object'&&String(pending.id)===String(id)&&Date.now()-pending.at<1800&&pending.key&&keys.includes(pending.key))?pending.key:keys[Number(index)]||null;
      const actual=stable?keys.indexOf(stable):Number(index);
      const result=priorSelectObjectKeyframe.call(this,id,actual);
      if(stable){
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
          try{
            if(!clip||!keys.includes(stable)||String(selectedClip)!==String(id))return;
            const nextIndex=keys.indexOf(stable);
            window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id:String(id),index:nextIndex,source:'pointer'};
            if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshObjectKeyframeTracks==='function')window.ethvelleRefreshObjectKeyframeTracks(id);
            if(typeof window.updateKeyButtonState==='function')window.updateKeyButtonState();
            if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
          }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle object keyframe selection repair:',err);}
        });
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv126Stable=true;window.selectObjectKeyframe=wrapped;
  }
  const priorSelectCharacterKeyframe=window.selectKeyframe;
  if(typeof priorSelectCharacterKeyframe==='function'&&!priorSelectCharacterKeyframe.__evv126Stable){
    const wrapped=function(index,ref){
      ref=ref||'main';
      const clip=typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(ref):(ref==='main'?activeClip:null);
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)?clip.keyframes:[];
      const pending=pendingKeyTap;pendingKeyTap=null;
      const stable=(pending&&pending.kind==='character'&&String(pending.ref)===String(ref)&&Date.now()-pending.at<1800&&pending.key&&keys.includes(pending.key))?pending.key:keys[Number(index)]||null;
      const actual=stable?keys.indexOf(stable):Number(index);
      const result=priorSelectCharacterKeyframe.call(this,actual,ref);
      if(stable){
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
          try{
            if(!clip||!keys.includes(stable)||String(selectedClip)!==String(ref))return;
            const nextIndex=keys.indexOf(stable);selectedKfIdx=nextIndex;
            const track=$(ref==='main'?'kfTrack':'kfTrack-'+ref);
            if(track){track.querySelectorAll('.kf-marker').forEach(m=>m.classList.toggle('active',Number(m.dataset.kfIdx)===nextIndex));}
            if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
            if(typeof window.updateKeyButtonState==='function')window.updateKeyButtonState();
          }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle character keyframe selection repair:',err);}
        });
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv126Stable=true;window.selectKeyframe=wrapped;
  }

  /* SINGLE OWNER for overlay trim-handle gestures. The old row renderer registered a
     second per-handle pointer state machine that could reapply its stale start/end snapshot
     after a drag had already committed. Intercept the handle at Window capture, update the
     model from one original snapshot, then rebuild the row exactly once on release. */
  let overlayTrim=null,trimMoveRaf=0,trimLatestX=0;
  /* v279: a handle only trims after a long press (gate). Until then the gesture is a plain
     scrub / row-scroll, exactly like sliding on the clip body. */
  let overlayTrimGate=null,overlayTrimPointerId=null,overlayTrimPendingHandle=null;
  function trimGroup(clip){return clip&&clip.type==='audio'?'audio':clip&&clip.type==='text'?'text':'clips';}
  function paintTrim(g,clientX){
    if(!g||!g.clip)return;
    const clip=g.clip,pps=Math.max(1,finite(typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?pxPerSecond():100,100));
    const delta=(finite(clientX,g.startX)-g.startX)/pps,speed=Math.max(.05,finite(g.speed,1));
    if(g.side==='left'){
      let start=clamp(g.origStart+delta,0,g.origEnd-MIN_CLIP_SEC);
      const duration=finite(clip.sourceDuration,0),offset=g.origSourceOffset;
      if((clip.type==='video'||clip.type==='audio')&&duration>0){
        const maxStart=g.origStart+Math.max(0,(duration-offset)/speed);
        start=Math.min(start,maxStart);
      }
      clip.trimStart=start;
      if(clip.type==='video'||clip.type==='audio')clip.sourceOffset=Math.max(0,g.origSourceOffset+(start-g.origStart)*speed);
    }else{
      let end=Math.max(g.origStart+MIN_CLIP_SEC,g.origEnd+delta);
      const duration=finite(clip.sourceDuration,0),offset=g.origSourceOffset;
      if((clip.type==='video'||clip.type==='audio')&&duration>0){
        const maxEnd=g.origStart+Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(duration-offset)/speed);
        end=Math.min(end,maxEnd);
      }
      clip.trimEnd=end;
    }
    const left=clip.trimStart*pps,width=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC*pps,(clip.trimEnd-clip.trimStart)*pps);
    if(g.el&&g.el.isConnected){g.el.style.left=left+'px';g.el.style.width=width+'px';}
    try{if(typeof window.applyOverlayLanes==='function')window.applyOverlayLanes(trimGroup(clip));}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof showTrimFrameBadge==='function')showTrimFrameBadge(g.handle,g.side==='left'?(clip.trimStart-g.origStart):(clip.trimEnd-g.origEnd));}catch(_e){}
    // The video filmstrip painter uses the clip's live trimStart/trimEnd/sourceOffset to
    // map each tile to its source frame. Schedule it during the gesture (not only after the
    // row is rebuilt on pointerup) so newly exposed frames appear while the edge is moving.
    if(clip.type==='video'){
      try{const thumbs=window.__evTimelineVideoThumbs;if(thumbs&&typeof thumbs.schedule==='function')thumbs.schedule();}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  function flushTrimMove(){if(!overlayTrim)return;trimMoveRaf=0;paintTrim(overlayTrim,trimLatestX);}
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    const handle=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip [data-ov-handle]'):null;
    if(!handle||e.isPrimary===false||e.button>0)return;
    const el=handle.closest('.overlay-clip'),id=el&&String(el.dataset.overlayId||'');
    const clip=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id):null;if(!clip)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if(isPlaying&&typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    if(typeof selectClipForChrome==='function')selectClipForChrome(id);
    try{if(typeof selectOverlayGroup==='function')selectOverlayGroup(trimGroup(clip));}catch(_e){}
    const side=String(handle.dataset.ovHandle||'right');
    overlayTrimPointerId=e.pointerId;overlayTrimPendingHandle=handle;
    overlayTrimGate=window.ethvelleMakeHandleHoldGate(function(armX){
      let liveEl=el;
      if(!liveEl.isConnected){
        try{liveEl=document.querySelector('#screen-editor #timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id="'+String(id).replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"]')||el;}catch(_e){liveEl=el;}
      }
      overlayTrim={pointerId:e.pointerId,clip,el:liveEl,handle,side,startX:armX,origStart:finite(clip.trimStart),origEnd:finite(clip.trimEnd),origSourceOffset:finite(clip.sourceOffset),speed:Math.max(.05,finite(clip.speed,1))};
      trimLatestX=armX;liveEl.classList.add('dragging');
      // Mark only this clip as a live trim preview. The shared filmstrip renderer can then
      // bypass its normal drag freeze for this strip without changing ordinary move-drag.
      liveEl.classList.add('ev-trim-preview-active');
      try{if(typeof armClipDrag==='function')armClipDrag();}catch(_e){}
    });
    overlayTrimGate.down(e);
    try{if(handle.setPointerCapture)handle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){
    if(overlayTrimGate&&overlayTrimPointerId===e.pointerId){
      const verdict=overlayTrimGate.move(e);
      if(verdict==='handled'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();return;}
    }
    const g=overlayTrim;if(!g||g.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    trimLatestX=e.clientX;
    if(!trimMoveRaf)trimMoveRaf=requestAnimationFrame(flushTrimMove);
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',function(e){
    if(overlayTrimGate&&overlayTrimPointerId===e.pointerId){
      const kind=overlayTrimGate.up(e);
      const pendingHandle=overlayTrimPendingHandle;
      overlayTrimGate=null;overlayTrimPointerId=null;overlayTrimPendingHandle=null;
      if(kind!=='trim'){
        // Plain tap, quick slide (scrub) or row scroll: nothing was trimmed, so nothing to commit.
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
        try{if(pendingHandle&&pendingHandle.hasPointerCapture&&pendingHandle.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))pendingHandle.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
        return;
      }
    }
    const g=overlayTrim;if(!g||g.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if(trimMoveRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(trimMoveRaf);trimMoveRaf=0;}
    trimLatestX=e.clientX;paintTrim(g,trimLatestX);overlayTrim=null;
    try{if(g.handle&&g.handle.hasPointerCapture&&g.handle.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))g.handle.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    if(g.el){g.el.classList.remove('dragging');g.el.classList.remove('ev-trim-preview-active');}
    try{if(typeof disarmClipDrag==='function')disarmClipDrag();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof hideTrimFrameBadge==='function')hideTrimFrameBadge();}catch(_e){}
    if(g.clip.trimEnd>timelineDuration-.3){timelineDuration=g.clip.trimEnd+1;if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();}
    if(g.clip.type==='audio'&&typeof drawWaveform==='function')drawWaveform(g.clip);
    if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();
    if(typeof selectClipForChrome==='function')selectClipForChrome(g.clip.id);
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(e){
    if(overlayTrimGate&&overlayTrimPointerId===e.pointerId){overlayTrimGate.cancel();overlayTrimGate=null;overlayTrimPointerId=null;overlayTrimPendingHandle=null;}
    const g=overlayTrim;if(!g||g.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    if(trimMoveRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(trimMoveRaf);trimMoveRaf=0;}
    g.clip.trimStart=g.origStart;g.clip.trimEnd=g.origEnd;g.clip.sourceOffset=g.origSourceOffset;overlayTrim=null;
    if(g.el){g.el.classList.remove('dragging');g.el.classList.remove('ev-trim-preview-active');}
    try{if(typeof disarmClipDrag==='function')disarmClipDrag();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof hideTrimFrameBadge==='function')hideTrimFrameBadge();}catch(_e){}
    if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();
    if(typeof selectClipForChrome==='function')selectClipForChrome(g.clip.id);
  },true);

  /* Normalize the Media control after any keyframe toolbar mirror rebuild. */
  function alignKeyframeMediaButton(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsKeyframe'),button=$('keyframeMediaAccessTool');
    if(!row||!button)return;
    button.style.setProperty('position','relative','important');button.style.setProperty('top','0','important');
    button.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');button.style.setProperty('margin','0','important');
    button.style.setProperty('align-self','stretch','important');button.style.setProperty('height','72px','important');
    button.style.setProperty('min-height','72px','important');button.style.setProperty('padding','10px 2px 8px','important');
  }
  const oldBottomSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldBottomSync==='function'&&!oldBottomSync.__evv126Align){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldBottomSync.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(alignKeyframeMediaButton);return r;};
    wrapped.__evv126Align=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  alignKeyframeMediaButton();
})();
</script>


<script id="ethvelle-v126-overlay-identity-guard">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* IDs are the identity key used by the layer renderer, selectors and save model.
     A repeated ID would render the same logical clip twice (often as an extra lane) while
     both visual copies still resolve to the first matching object. Keep the first model
     only; intentionally duplicated media still works because real duplicates have new IDs. */
  const previousRender=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  if(typeof previousRender==='function'&&!previousRender.__evv126UniqueIds){
    const wrapped=function(){
      if(Array.isArray(overlays)){
        const seen=new Set(),kept=[];
        for(const clip of overlays){
          if(!clip||clip.id==null){kept.push(clip);continue;}
          const id=String(clip.id);
          if(seen.has(id))continue;
          seen.add(id);kept.push(clip);
        }
        if(kept.length!==overlays.length){overlays.length=0;for(const clip of kept)overlays.push(clip);}
      }
      return previousRender.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv126UniqueIds=true;window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v128-model-parts-held-items-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safe=v=>String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const currentAnimClip=()=>{
    if(typeof selectedClip!=='string'||!(selectedClip==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(selectedClip)))return null;
    return typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;
  };
  const currentChar=()=>typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;
  const charParts=c=>c&&c.sheet&&c.sheet.parts?c.sheet.parts:{};
  const actionDefaultsV128=()=>({expression:'none',mouth:'preset',blink:'auto',eyes:'auto',leftHand:'none',rightHand:'none'});
  const normalizeActionsV128=input=>{
    const a={...actionDefaultsV128(),...(input&&typeof input==='object'?input:{})};
    if(!['none','sad','angry','shock','laugh'].includes(a.expression))a.expression='none';
    if(!['preset','talk','closed','hold'].includes(a.mouth))a.mouth='preset';
    if(!['auto','ambient','off'].includes(a.blink))a.blink='auto';
    if(!['auto','open','closed'].includes(a.eyes))a.eyes='auto';
    ['leftHand','rightHand'].forEach(k=>{if(!['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold'].includes(a[k]))a[k]='none';});
    return a;
  };
  function choiceTile(key,label,src,active,handler,icon){
    return `<button type="button" class="evpa-choice ${active?'active':''}" aria-pressed="${active?'true':'false'}" onclick="${handler}"><span class="evpa-choice-preview">${src?`<img src="${safe(src)}" alt="">`:`<span class="evpa-choice-icon">${icon||'◌'}</span>`}</span><span class="evpa-choice-label">${safe(label)}</span></button>`;
  }
  function faceChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const parts=charParts(c),items=[
      ['none','Original',parts.head,'🙂'],
      ...(parts.head_sad?[['sad','Sad',parts.head_sad,'🙁']]:[]),
      ...(parts.head_angry?[['angry','Angry',parts.head_angry,'😠']]:[]),
      ...(parts.head_shock?[['shock','Surprised',parts.head_shock,'😮']]:[]),
      ...(parts.head_laugh?[['laugh','Laughing',parts.head_laugh,'😄']]:[])
    ];
    return items.map(([v,l,part,ico])=>choiceTile('face-'+v,l,part&&part.src,a.expression===v,`updateClipFaceAction('expression','${v}')`,ico)).join('');
  }
  function eyeChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const p=charParts(c),items=[choiceTile('eyes-auto','Auto',p.head&&p.head.src,a.eyes==='auto',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','auto')",'◉')];
    if(p.head_open)items.push(choiceTile('eyes-open','Eyes open',p.head_open.src,a.eyes==='open',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','open')",'◉'));
    if(p.head_blink)items.push(choiceTile('eyes-closed','Eyes closed',p.head_blink.src,a.eyes==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('eyes','closed')",'—'));
    return items.join('');
  }
  function blinkChoiceGrid(a){
    return [
      choiceTile('blink-auto','Clip blinking',null,a.blink==='auto',"updateClipFaceAction('blink','auto')",'◉'),
      choiceTile('blink-ambient','Ambient',null,a.blink==='ambient',"updateClipFaceAction('blink','ambient')",'◌'),
      choiceTile('blink-off','Off',null,a.blink==='off',"updateClipFaceAction('blink','off')",'—')
    ].join('');
  }
  function mouthChoiceGrid(c,a){
    const p=charParts(c),type=String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid'),species=type==='animal'||type==='bird';
    return [
      choiceTile('mouth-preset','Animation',p.mouth_closed&&p.mouth_closed.src,a.mouth==='preset',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','preset')",'◡'),
      ...(p.mouth_open?[choiceTile('mouth-talk','Talk loop',p.mouth_open.src,a.mouth==='talk',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','talk')",'◉')]:[]),
      choiceTile('mouth-closed','Closed',p.mouth_closed&&p.mouth_closed.src,a.mouth==='closed',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','closed')",'—'),
      ...(species?[choiceTile('mouth-hold','Hold item',p.mouth_hold&&p.mouth_hold.src,a.mouth==='hold',"updateClipFaceAction('mouth','hold')",'◒')]:[])
    ].join('');
  }
  function mouthItemSection(c){
    const type=String(c&&(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');if(type!=='animal'&&type!=='bird')return '';
    const item=c.mouthProp,scale=Math.round((Number(item&&item.scale)||1)*100),rotation=Math.round(Number(item&&item.rotation)||0),x=Math.round(Number(item&&item.offsetX)||0),y=Math.round(Number(item&&item.offsetY)||0);
    return `<section class="evpa-section evca-mouth-item-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Hold an item with the ${type==='bird'?'beak':'mouth'}</p><input id="evcaMouthItemFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*" hidden onchange="setCharacterMouthItemFile(this)"><button type="button" class="evpa-prop-upload" onclick="document.getElementById('evcaMouthItemFile').click()">＋ ${item?'Replace mouth item':'Add mouth item'}</button>${item?`<div class="evca-mouth-item-preview"><img src="${safe(item.src)}" alt="Mouth-held item"><button type="button" onclick="removeCharacterMouthItem()">Remove</button></div>`:''}<div class="evca-mouth-item-controls" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}><label>Size <output id="evcaMouthScaleVal">${scale}%</output><input type="range" min="20" max="220" value="${scale}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('scale',Number(this.value)/100)"></label><label>Rotate <output id="evcaMouthRotationVal">${rotation}°</output><input type="range" min="-180" max="180" value="${rotation}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('rotation',Number(this.value))"></label><label>Move X <output id="evcaMouthXVal">${x}</output><input type="range" min="-100" max="100" value="${x}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('offsetX',Number(this.value))"></label><label>Move Y <output id="evcaMouthYVal">${y}</output><input type="range" min="-100" max="100" value="${y}" oninput="setCharacterMouthItemValue('offsetY',Number(this.value))"></label></div></section>`;
  }
  window.setCharacterMouthItemFile=function(input){
    const file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(input)input.value='';if(!file)return;const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;
    const type=String(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid');if(type!=='animal'&&type!=='bird')return;
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read the mouth item image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not decode the mouth item image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};img.onload=()=>{
      const max=640,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio)),cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=w;cv.height=h;const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
      c.mouthProp={src:cv.toDataURL('image/png'),name:file.name||'Mouth item',scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false};
      const clip=currentAnimClip();if(clip){const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions={...a,mouth:'hold'};}
      if(!c.expr)c.expr=typeof sheetExprFresh==='function'?sheetExprFresh():{};c.expr.mouth='hold';
      if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();window.openClipCharacterActions();
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Item attached to the '+(type==='bird'?'beak':'mouth'),{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1500});
    };img.src=String(reader.result||'');};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };
  window.setCharacterMouthItemValue=function(field,value){const c=currentChar(),item=c&&c.mouthProp;if(!item||!['scale','rotation','offsetX','offsetY'].includes(field)||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return;item[field]=Number(value);const id={scale:'evcaMouthScaleVal',rotation:'evcaMouthRotationVal',offsetX:'evcaMouthXVal',offsetY:'evcaMouthYVal'}[field],out=$(id);if(out)out.textContent=field==='scale'?Math.round(item.scale*100)+'%':field==='rotation'?Math.round(item.rotation)+'°':String(Math.round(item[field]));if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();};
  window.removeCharacterMouthItem=function(){const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;c.mouthProp=null;const clip=currentAnimClip();if(clip){const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions={...a,mouth:'closed'};}if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();window.openClipCharacterActions();};
  function handChoiceGrid(c,a,side){
    const p=charParts(c),field=side==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand',base='hand'+side;
    const items=[['none','Natural',p[base],'✋'],['fist','Fist',p[base+'_fist'],'✊'],['point','Point',p[base+'_point'],'☝'],['thumb','Thumbs up',p[base+'_thumb'],'👍'],['palm','Open palm',p[base+'_palm'],'🖐'],['hold','Holding grip',p[base+'_hold'],'🤏']];
    return items.filter(([v,l,part])=>v==='none'||!!part).map(([v,l,part,ico])=>choiceTile('hand'+side+'-'+v,l,part&&part.src,a[field]===v,`updateClipFaceAction('${field}','${v}')`,ico)).join('');
  }
  function ensureFaceBackdrop(){
    let el=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');
    if(!el){const host=$('screen-editor')||document.body;el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop';el.className='modal-backdrop evca-char-actions-backdrop';host.appendChild(el);el.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===el)closeClipCharacterActions();});}
    return el;
  }
  window.openClipCharacterActions=function(){
    const clip=currentAnimClip(),c=currentChar();
    if(!clip||!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character animation clip first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1700});return;}
    const p=charParts(c),a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions=a;
    const type=String(c.modelType||(c.sheet&&c.sheet.modelType)||'humanoid'),species=type==='animal'||type==='bird';
    const back=ensureFaceBackdrop(),oldSheet=back.querySelector('.modal-sheet'),oldScroll=oldSheet?oldSheet.scrollTop:0;
    const title=species?'Face &amp; mouth actions':'Model parts &amp; hands';
    const moveNote=species?`<p class="evca-species-movement-note">${type==='bird'?'Wing flaps, hops and bird movement':'Walking, running and four-legged movement'} are controlled by the matching animation presets in the Animations section.</p>`:'';
    const handSections=species?'':`<section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Left hand</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${handChoiceGrid(c,a,'L')}</div></section><section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Right hand</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${handChoiceGrid(c,a,'R')}</div></section>`;
    back.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-sheet evca-char-actions-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${species?'Face and mouth actions':'Model parts and hand gestures'}">
      <div class="sheet-handle"></div><p class="sheet-title">${title}</p>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Face expression</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${faceChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Eyes</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${eyeChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Blink mode</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${blinkChoiceGrid(a)}</div></section>
      <section class="evpa-section"><p class="evpa-section-title">Mouth</p><div class="evpa-choice-grid">${mouthChoiceGrid(c,a)}</div></section>
      ${mouthItemSection(c)}${moveNote}${handSections}
      <button type="button" class="evpa-close-btn" onclick="closeClipCharacterActions()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    back.classList.add('active');
    if(oldScroll)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const sheet=back.querySelector('.modal-sheet');if(sheet)sheet.scrollTop=oldScroll;});
  };
  window.closeClipCharacterActions=function(){const el=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');};
  window.updateClipFaceAction=function(field,value){
    const clip=currentAnimClip(),c=currentChar();if(!clip||!c)return;
    const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);
    const allowed={expression:['none','sad','angry','shock','laugh'],mouth:['preset','talk','closed','hold'],blink:['auto','ambient','off'],eyes:['auto','open','closed'],leftHand:['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold'],rightHand:['none','point','thumb','palm','fist','hold']};
    if(!allowed[field]||!allowed[field].includes(value))return;
    if(field==='expression'&&value!=='none'&&!charParts(c)['head_'+value])return;
    if(field==='eyes'&&value==='open'&&!charParts(c).head_open)return;
    if(field==='eyes'&&value==='closed'&&!charParts(c).head_blink)return;
    if((field==='leftHand'||field==='rightHand')&&value!=='none'){
      const side=field==='leftHand'?'L':'R';if(!charParts(c)['hand'+side+'_'+value])return;
    }
    clip.faceActions={...a,[field]:value};
    if(typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();else if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
    window.openClipCharacterActions();
  };

  window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor=function(){
    const c=currentChar();if(!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    if(c.sheet&&typeof edOpenFromChar==='function'){edOpenFromChar(activeCharIdx);return;}
    if(c.type==='custom'&&c.parts&&typeof openCustomCharBuilder==='function'){openCustomCharBuilder(true);return;}
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('This character has no editable image parts. Add a 14-part model first.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2300});
  };

  let propSide='R';
  function ensurePropsBackdrop(){
    let el=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');
    if(!el){const host=$('screen-editor')||document.body;el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop';el.className='modal-backdrop evca-char-actions-backdrop';host.appendChild(el);el.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===el)closeCharacterPropsPanel();});}
    return el;
  }
  function ensurePropState(c){if(!c.handProps||typeof c.handProps!=='object')c.handProps={L:null,R:null};if(!('L'in c.handProps))c.handProps.L=null;if(!('R'in c.handProps))c.handProps.R=null;return c.handProps;}
  function drawPropsPanelPreview(){
    const target=$('evpaPropStagePreview'),source=$('stickCanvas');
    if(!target||!source||!source.width||!source.height)return;
    const ctx=target.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,target.width,target.height);
    const ratio=Math.min(target.width/source.width,target.height/source.height);
    const w=source.width*ratio,h=source.height*ratio;
    try{ctx.drawImage(source,(target.width-w)/2,(target.height-h)/2,w,h);}catch(_e){}
  }
  function refreshPropsPreview(){requestAnimationFrame(drawPropsPanelPreview);}
  function renderPropsPanel(){
    const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;const props=ensurePropState(c),item=props[propSide];
    const preview=item?`<img src="${safe(item.src)}" alt="Held object preview">`:`<span class="evpa-prop-empty">No object</span>`;
    const n=(v,d)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
    const scale=n(item&&item.scale,1),rotation=n(item&&item.rotation,0),x=n(item&&item.offsetX,0),y=n(item&&item.offsetY,0);
    const back=ensurePropsBackdrop();
    back.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-sheet evpa-props-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Held items">
      <div class="sheet-handle"></div><p class="sheet-title">Held items</p>
      <div class="evpa-action-side"><button type="button" class="${propSide==='L'?'active':''}" onclick="setCharacterPropSide('L')">Left hand</button><button type="button" class="${propSide==='R'?'active':''}" onclick="setCharacterPropSide('R')">Right hand</button></div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-preview" id="evpaPropPreview">${preview}</div>
      <input id="evpaPropFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*" hidden onchange="setCharacterHeldPropFile(this)">
      <button type="button" class="evpa-prop-upload" onclick="document.getElementById('evpaPropFile').click()">＋ ${item?'Replace object':'Add object image'}</button>
      <div class="evpa-prop-stage-wrap"><div class="evpa-prop-stage-title">On-character preview</div><canvas id="evpaPropStagePreview" width="270" height="480" aria-label="Character with held object preview"></canvas></div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-controls" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Size <output id="evpaPropScaleVal">${Math.round(scale*100)}%</output></label><input type="range" min="20" max="250" step="1" value="${Math.round(scale*100)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('scale',Number(this.value)/100)"></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Rotate <output id="evpaPropRotationVal">${Math.round(rotation)}°</output></label><input type="range" min="-180" max="180" step="1" value="${Math.round(rotation)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('rotation',Number(this.value))"></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Move X <output id="evpaPropXVal">${Math.round(x)}</output></label><input type="range" id="evpaPropXInput" min="-100" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.round(x)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('offsetX',Number(this.value))"></div>
        <div class="evpa-prop-field"><label>Move Y <output id="evpaPropYVal">${Math.round(y)}</output></label><input type="range" id="evpaPropYInput" min="-100" max="100" step="1" value="${Math.round(y)}" oninput="setCharacterHeldPropValue('offsetY',Number(this.value))"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="evpa-prop-toggle-row" ${item?'':'style="opacity:.45;pointer-events:none"'}><button type="button" class="${item&&item.flipX?'active':''}" onclick="toggleCharacterHeldProp('flipX')">Flip horizontal</button><button type="button" class="${item&&item.flipY?'active':''}" onclick="toggleCharacterHeldProp('flipY')">Flip vertical</button></div>
      ${item?'<button type="button" class="evpa-prop-remove" onclick="removeCharacterHeldProp()">Remove object</button>':''}
      <button type="button" class="evpa-close-btn" onclick="closeCharacterPropsPanel()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    back.classList.add('active');
    refreshPropsPreview();
  }
  window.openCharacterPropsPanel=function(){
    const c=currentChar();if(!c){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a character first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    ensurePropState(c);propSide='R';renderPropsPanel();
  };
  window.closeCharacterPropsPanel=function(){const el=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');};
  window.setCharacterPropSide=function(side){if(!['L','R'].includes(side))return;propSide=side;renderPropsPanel();};
  window.setCharacterHeldPropValue=function(field,value){
    const c=currentChar(),item=c&&ensurePropState(c)[propSide];if(!item)return;
    if(!['scale','rotation','offsetX','offsetY'].includes(field)||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return;
    item[field]=Number(value);
    const output={scale:'evpaPropScaleVal',rotation:'evpaPropRotationVal',offsetX:'evpaPropXVal',offsetY:'evpaPropYVal'}[field];
    const label=$(output);if(label)label.textContent=field==='scale'?Math.round(item.scale*100)+'%':(field==='rotation'?Math.round(item.rotation)+'°':Math.round(item[field]));
    if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();refreshPropsPreview();
  };
  window.toggleCharacterHeldProp=function(field){
    if(!['flipX','flipY'].includes(field))return;const c=currentChar(),item=c&&ensurePropState(c)[propSide];if(!item)return;
    item[field]=!item[field];if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderPropsPanel();
  };
  window.removeCharacterHeldProp=function(){const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;ensurePropState(c)[propSide]=null;if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderPropsPanel();};
  window.setCharacterHeldPropFile=function(input){
    const file=input.files&&input.files[0];input.value='';if(!file)return;
    const c=currentChar();if(!c)return;
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{
      const source=String(reader.result||'');const img=new Image();
      img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not decode the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
      img.onload=()=>{
        const max=640,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
        const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;
        const cx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!cx){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object image could not be prepared',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
        cx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);cx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
        const data=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
        ensurePropState(c)[propSide]={src:data,name:file.name||'Object',scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false};
        const clip=currentAnimClip(),parts=charParts(c),field=propSide==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand';
        if(clip&&parts['hand'+propSide+'_hold']){const a=normalizeActionsV128(clip.faceActions);clip.faceActions={...a,[field]:'hold'};}
        if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();renderPropsPanel();
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object attached to '+(propSide==='L'?'left':'right')+' hand',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
      };
      img.src=source;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  // Draw an uploaded object over its hand in the same local bone-space transform as the character.
  // Because drawCharacterToContext calls this hook, the object is visible both on the editor stage and export frames.
  window.ethvelleDrawCharacterProps=function(targetCtx,char,boneStart,boneEnd){
    const props=char&&char.handProps;if(!props)return;
    const cache=window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache||(window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache=new Map());
    ['L','R'].forEach(side=>{
      const item=props[side];if(!item||!item.src)return;
      const key='hand'+side;let img=cache.get(item.src);
      if(!img){img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();};img.onerror=()=>{};cache.set(item.src,img);img.src=item.src;}
      if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth)return;
      let start,end;try{start=boneStart(key);end=boneEnd(key);}catch(_e){return;}
      if(!start||!end||![start.x,start.y,end.x,end.y].every(Number.isFinite))return;
      const dx=end.x-start.x,dy=end.y-start.y,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)-Math.PI/2+(Number(item.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
      const handX=start.x+dx*.52,handY=start.y+dy*.52;
      const bodyKeys=['spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','thighL','shinL','thighR','shinR'];
      const bodyLen=bodyKeys.reduce((sum,k)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(char.bones&&char.bones[k]&&char.bones[k].length)||0),0)||Number(stageH)||420;
      const h=Math.max(24,Math.min(190,bodyLen*.235*Math.max(.2,Math.min(2.5,Number(item.scale)||1))));
      const aspect=img.naturalWidth/Math.max(1,img.naturalHeight),w=Math.max(16,Math.min(190,h*aspect));
      targetCtx.save();targetCtx.translate(handX,handY);targetCtx.rotate(angle);
      targetCtx.translate(Number(item.offsetX)||0,Number(item.offsetY)||0);
      targetCtx.scale(item.flipX?-1:1,item.flipY?-1:1);
      targetCtx.drawImage(img,-w*.5,-h*.55,w,h);
      targetCtx.restore();
    });
  };

  // Force the latest contextual toolbar state after every existing synchronizer (many versions wrap it).
  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSync==='function'&&!priorSync.__ethvelleV128ModelProps){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=priorSync.apply(this,arguments);const isStick=!!currentAnimClip();
      const face=$('clipCharacterActionsTool'),model=$('clipModelPartsTool'),held=$('clipHeldItemsTool');
      if(face)face.style.display=isStick?'flex':'none';if(model)model.style.display=isStick?'flex':'none';if(held)held.style.display=isStick?'flex':'none';return r;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV128ModelProps=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{if(window.syncBottomToolsMode)window.syncBottomToolsMode();},{once:true});
  else if(window.syncBottomToolsMode)window.syncBottomToolsMode();
})();
</script>
