        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" aria-label="Clear animation search" onclick="clearCharacterAnimationSearch()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      <div class="anim-list" id="animListBody"></div>`;
  }
  window.filterCharacterAnimations=function(value){animationSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase();const bar=$('evcaAnimSearchBar');if(bar)bar.classList.toggle('has-query',!!animationSearchQuery);window.renderAnimList();};
  window.clearCharacterAnimationSearch=function(){animationSearchQuery='';const input=$('evcaAnimSearchInput');if(input)input.value='';const bar=$('evcaAnimSearchBar');if(bar)bar.classList.remove('has-query');window.renderAnimList();if(input)input.focus();};
  window.renderAnimList=function(){
    const container=$('animListBody');if(!container)return;
    const selectedAnimKey=(()=>{if(selectedClip==='main'&&activeClip)return activeClip.animKey;if(typeof selectedClip==='string'&&selectedClip.startsWith('seg')){const idx=parseInt(selectedClip.slice(3),10);return splitStickmanSegments[idx]?.animKey||null;}if(selectedClip==null)return activeClip?.animKey||null;return null;})();
    const activeType=activeAnimationModelType();if(!animationCategory)animationCategory=activeType;
    const category=animationCategory,compatible=category===activeType;
    const query=animationSearchQuery||String(($('evcaAnimSearchInput')||{}).value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const keys=Object.keys(animPresets||{}).filter(key=>{const p=animPresets[key]||{},type=p.modelType||'humanoid';return type===category&&(!query||String(p.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(query)||String(key).toLowerCase().includes(query));});
    document.querySelectorAll('.evca-animation-categories [data-anim-type]').forEach(btn=>{const active=btn.dataset.animType===category;btn.classList.toggle('active',active);btn.setAttribute('aria-selected',active?'true':'false');});
    const bar=$('evcaAnimSearchBar');if(bar)bar.classList.toggle('has-query',!!query);
    const notice='';
    if(!keys.length){container.innerHTML=notice+'<div class="evca-anim-empty">No animations found. Try another name.</div>';return;}
    container.innerHTML=notice+keys.map(key=>{
      const p=animPresets[key];const applied=key===selectedAnimKey,previewing=key===previewAnimKey;
      const required=getAnimationRequiredPlan(key),locked=isAnimationLocked(key);
      const action=!compatible?'':locked&&!applied?`<button type="button" class="anim-lock-btn" onclick="event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();openAnimationUpgrade('${safe(required.id)}')">🔒 ${safe(required.name||'Paid')}</button>`:`<button type="button" class="anim-toggle ${applied?'on':''}" aria-pressed="${applied?'true':'false'}" title="${applied?'Turn off':'Add animation'}" onclick="event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();toggleApplyAnim('${safe(key)}')"></button>`;
      return `<div class="anim-item ${previewing?'selected':''}" role="button" tabindex="0" onclick="previewAnim('${safe(key)}')" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();previewAnim('${safe(key)}')}"><div class="anim-play-mini"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/></svg></div><div class="anim-item-name">${safe(p.name||key)}</div><div class="anim-item-dur">${Number(p.duration||1).toFixed(1)}s</div>${action}</div>`;
    }).join('');
  };

  /* Add a small custom-part upload button to each part tile in the Model Lab. */
  function ensureBodyPartInput(){
    let input=$('edBodyPartFile');if(input)return input;
    input=document.createElement('input');input.type='file';input.id='edBodyPartFile';input.accept='image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*';input.hidden=true;
    input.addEventListener('change',()=>window.edHandleBodyPartFile(input));
    const host=$('sheetEd')||document.body;host.appendChild(input);return input;
  }
  window.edUploadBodyPart=function(key){
    if(typeof ED==='undefined'||!ED||!ED.st||!ED.st[key]){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Open the model editor first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    window.__ethvellePendingCustomPartKey=key;const input=ensureBodyPartInput();input.value='';input.click();
  };
  window.edHandleBodyPartFile=function(input){
    const file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0],key=window.__ethvellePendingCustomPartKey;if(input)input.value='';window.__ethvellePendingCustomPartKey=null;if(!file||!key)return;
    if(!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif|avif)/i.test(file.type||'')){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Choose a PNG, JPG or WebP image',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read this custom part image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not open this custom part image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};img.onload=()=>{
      try{
        const max=1024,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Canvas unavailable');ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
        if(typeof edVariantRemoveFlatBackground==='function')edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(canvas);
        const art=edMakeArt(canvas,key,ED.modelType);if(!art||!art.alpha||!art.alpha.some(v=>v)){throw new Error('No visible pixels found');}
        const oldArt=ED.arts[ED.assign[key]]||ED.arts[key]||null,st=ED.st[key];
        edPush();
        const artKey='custom_'+String(key).replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi,'_')+'_'+Date.now().toString(36);
        art.src=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');art.customName=file.name||'Custom part';art.thumb=null;
        ED.arts[artKey]=art;ED.assign[key]=artKey;
        if(st){
          if(oldArt&&oldArt.w&&oldArt.h){const fx=oldArt.w/Math.max(1,art.w),fy=oldArt.h/Math.max(1,art.h),uniform=Math.sqrt(Math.max(.0001,fx*fy));st.s=(Number(st.s)||1)*uniform;st.wx=(Number(st.wx)||1)*(fx/uniform);st.hy=(Number(st.hy)||1)*(fy/uniform);}
          st.pvx=Number(art.dpx)||art.w/2;st.pvy=Number(art.dpy)||art.h/2;
        }
        ED.sel=key;edSyncUI();edDraw();
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Custom '+(typeof sheetLabel==='function'?sheetLabel(key):key)+' part added',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1700});
      }catch(err){console.error('Custom model part upload failed',err);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(err&&err.message==='No visible pixels found'?'No visible artwork found in that image':'Could not add this custom part',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});}
    };img.src=String(reader.result||'');};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };
  const originalEdSync=window.edSyncUI;
  if(typeof originalEdSync==='function'&&!originalEdSync.__ethvelleCustomPartGridTile){
    const wrappedEdSync=function(){
      const result=originalEdSync.apply(this,arguments),box=$('edLayers');if(!box)return result;
      /* Remove any stale overlay-style controls left by an earlier render, then append one grid tile. */
      box.querySelectorAll(':scope > .ed-chip-wrap,:scope > .ed-chip-plus,:scope > .ed-chip-custom').forEach(el=>el.remove());
      const add=document.createElement('button');add.type='button';add.className='ed-chip ed-chip-custom';
      add.setAttribute('aria-label','Add custom body-part artwork');add.title='Add custom body-part artwork';
      add.innerHTML='<span class="th"><span class="ed-chip-custom-icon" aria-hidden="true">+</span></span><span class="n">Add part</span>';
      add.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        const selected=(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.sel)||((box.querySelector('.ed-chip.is-on')||{}).dataset||{}).k;
        if(!selected){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a body part first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
        window.edUploadBodyPart(selected);
      });
      box.appendChild(add);return result;
    };
    wrappedEdSync.__ethvelleCustomPartGridTile=true;window.edSyncUI=wrappedEdSync;
  }

  /* Remove the old top title; add plus controls by the image-part sections. */
  window.openCustomPartEditorFromAction=function(){window.closeClipCharacterActions();window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor();};
  const priorOpenActions=window.openClipCharacterActions;
  if(typeof priorOpenActions==='function'&&!priorOpenActions.__ethvelleV130PartHeadings){
    const wrappedActions=function(){const result=priorOpenActions.apply(this,arguments);const back=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');if(!back)return result;
      const title=back.querySelector('.modal-sheet>.sheet-title');if(title)title.remove();
      back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-section').forEach(section=>{
        const heading=section.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-section-title');if(!heading)return;
        const label=heading.textContent.trim();if(!section.querySelector('.evpa-section-add')&&!/^blink/i.test(label)){
          const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='evpa-section-heading';section.insertBefore(row,heading);row.appendChild(heading);
          const add=document.createElement('button');add.type='button';add.className='evpa-section-add';add.textContent='+';add.title='Add custom artwork';add.setAttribute('aria-label','Add custom '+label+' artwork');add.addEventListener('click',window.openCustomPartEditorFromAction);row.appendChild(add);
        }
      });return result;
    };
    wrappedActions.__ethvelleV130PartHeadings=true;window.openClipCharacterActions=wrappedActions;
  }
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle v133: persistent keyframe rails + complete single-action undo/redo history. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v133-persistent-keyframes-css">
  /* Keyframe diamonds remain painted for selected and unselected clips alike. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .object-keyframe-track{
    visibility:visible!important;opacity:1!important;pointer-events:none!important;
  }
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .object-keyframe-track .kf-marker{
    pointer-events:auto!important;
  }
  /* Other character rows are visual previews until that character is activated. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner .anim-clip-readonly .ev-readonly-kf-marker{
    pointer-events:none!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v133-complete-history-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV133HistoryInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV133HistoryInstalled=true;

  const editor=document.getElementById('screen-editor');
  const activePointers=new Set();
  const originalPushHistory=window.pushHistory;
  let transactionOpen=false;
  let commitTimer=null;
  // Invalidates delayed commits when Undo/Redo or project reset is requested. Without this,
  // a pending pointer/input timer could run just after Undo and accidentally erase redo.
  let transactionEpoch=0;

  function isEditorEvent(e){
    if(!editor||!e||!e.target)return false;
    if(!editor.contains(e.target))return false;
    // Undo and redo must not record themselves as new edits.
    if(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#undoBtn,#redoBtn'))return false;
    return true;
  }
  function clearCommitTimer(){
    if(commitTimer!==null){clearTimeout(commitTimer);commitTimer=null;}
  }
  function beginTransaction(){
    clearCommitTimer();
    if(transactionOpen)return;
    try{
      // Project opening already seeds history with resetHistory(). Only seed here as a
      // safety net for a newly-created editor; avoid serializing large media projects on
      // every ordinary tap/drag start when the previous action is already committed.
      if(typeof pushHistory==='function'&&!restoringHistory&&typeof historyIndex!=='undefined'&&historyIndex<0)pushHistory();
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle history baseline:',err);}
    transactionOpen=true;
  }
  function commitTransaction(delay){
    if(!transactionOpen)return;
    clearCommitTimer();
    const epoch=transactionEpoch;
    commitTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      commitTimer=null;
      if(epoch!==transactionEpoch)return;
      // Pointer capture is occasionally cancelled by Android WebView while a panel is
      // rebuilding. Retry briefly rather than leaving the transaction permanently open.
      if(activePointers.size){commitTransaction(90);return;}
      try{
        // Bypass the transaction-aware wrapper below for this one final snapshot.
        if(typeof originalPushHistory==='function'&&!restoringHistory)originalPushHistory();
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle history commit:',err);}
      transactionOpen=false;
    },Math.max(0,Number(delay)||0));
  }
  function flushPendingTransaction(){
    clearCommitTimer();
    const hadPending=transactionOpen;
    activePointers.clear();
    transactionOpen=false;
    transactionEpoch++;
    // Commit the just-finished edit BEFORE moving the history cursor. This guarantees
    // Undo creates a redo entry even if the normal gesture debounce had not fired yet.
    if(hadPending&&!restoringHistory&&typeof originalPushHistory==='function'){
      try{originalPushHistory();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle history flush:',err);}
    }
    if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
  }
  window.__ethvelleV139FlushHistory=flushPendingTransaction;
  window.__ethvelleV139ResetHistoryTransaction=function(){
    clearCommitTimer();activePointers.clear();transactionOpen=false;transactionEpoch++;
  };
  function ensurePendingCommit(delay){
    // Persistence may happen after an async file decode/thumbnail operation and therefore
    // outside the original pointer gesture. In that case the current history head is the
    // pre-change state, so just append the resulting state once the burst settles.
    if(!transactionOpen)transactionOpen=true;
    commitTransaction(delay);
  }

  // Default persistence paths may fire repeatedly while a slider is moving. Suppress those
  // intermediate pushes during an active interaction; the transaction commits exactly one
  // final state when the gesture/input burst ends.
  if(typeof originalPushHistory==='function'&&!originalPushHistory.__ethvelleV133History){
    const wrappedPushHistory=function(){
      if(transactionOpen&&!restoringHistory)return;
      return originalPushHistory.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedPushHistory.__ethvelleV133History=true;
    window.pushHistory=wrappedPushHistory;
  }

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(!isEditorEvent(e))return;
    beginTransaction();
    if(e.pointerId!==undefined)activePointers.add(e.pointerId);
  },true);

  const finishPointer=e=>{
    // Pointer capture can be released after the finger leaves the editor; still finish the
    // matching gesture so history never remains stuck open.
    if(e.pointerId===undefined||!activePointers.has(e.pointerId))return;
    activePointers.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(activePointers.size===0)commitTransaction(70);
  };
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',finishPointer,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishPointer,true);

  // Clicks cover keyboard activation and programmatic .click() controls; capture runs before
  // inline onclick handlers, and the delayed commit runs after those handlers have mutated data.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!isEditorEvent(e))return;
    beginTransaction();
    commitTransaction(80);
  },true);

  // Range sliders are one history step per completed drag. Keyboard/text edits are grouped
  // into one step after the user pauses, rather than one undo entry per character/frame.
  document.addEventListener('input',e=>{
    if(!isEditorEvent(e))return;
    beginTransaction();
    commitTransaction(260);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(!isEditorEvent(e))return;
    beginTransaction();
    commitTransaction(100);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(!isEditorEvent(e))return;
    beginTransaction();
    commitTransaction(320);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{
    if(!isEditorEvent(e))return;
    if(transactionOpen)commitTransaction(240);
  },true);

  // Some editor tools intentionally save with skipHistory:true while changing live values.
  // Keep their persistence behavior, but still commit the final state once the interaction
  // (or async update burst) has finished. A pointer gesture already owns its transaction.
  const originalPersist=window.persistProjectState;
  if(typeof originalPersist==='function'&&!originalPersist.__ethvelleV133History){
    const wrappedPersist=function(opts){
      const result=originalPersist.apply(this,arguments);
      if(opts&&opts.skipHistory===true&&!restoringHistory){
        ensurePendingCommit(180);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedPersist.__ethvelleV133History=true;
    window.persistProjectState=wrappedPersist;
  }

  if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle v138: stable video audio clock, held scrub frames, and exact post-scrub decoder sync. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v138-scrub-frame-hold-css">
  #screen-editor #overlayMediaLayer > canvas.ev-v138-scrub-hold-frame{
    position:absolute!important;inset:0!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;
    object-fit:contain!important;object-position:center!important;pointer-events:none!important;
    display:block!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v138-scrub-frame-hold-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV138VideoStabilityInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV138VideoStabilityInstalled=true;

  const baseRender=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof baseRender!=='function')return;
  const heldById=new Map();

  /* v207 - blank canvas while dragging along video clips.
     While a finger drags the timeline, Android keeps every <video> in a permanent "seeking"
     state and its surface paints nothing, so the stage showed only the white background.
     The old hold frame could only be captured while the decoder was idle, was keyed to one
     <video> element (a clip that had just become active had none), and was removed the moment
     the element reported "ready" - often a frame before it had actually painted.
     Now: (1) pictures are validated before they replace a good hold, (2) a small cache of
     recently decoded pictures seeds a hold for any clip that has no picture of its own yet,
     (3) a hold is captured again on every 'seeked', and (4) it is released only after the
     video has really presented a frame. */
  const frameCache=[];
  const FRAME_CACHE_MAX=18,FRAME_CACHE_SIDE=480,HOLD_RELEASE_GRACE_MS=170;
  let probeCtx=null;
  try{const pc=document.createElement('canvas');pc.width=4;pc.height=4;probeCtx=pc.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});}catch(_e){probeCtx=null;}

  function overlayList(){
    return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
  }
  function activeVideosAt(t){
    return overlayList().filter(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&!o.reversePlayback&&
      Number(t)>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&Number(t)<Number(o.trimEnd||0));
  }
  function getVideo(id){
    try{return typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls[String(id)]||null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function overlayForVideo(el){
    try{
      if(typeof overlayMediaEls==='undefined'||!overlayMediaEls)return null;
      const ids=Object.keys(overlayMediaEls);
      for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
        if(overlayMediaEls[ids[i]]===el)return overlayList().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(ids[i]))||null;
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    return null;
  }
  function currentTimeSec(){
    return typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0:0;
  }
  // A decoder that just finished a seek can still hand back an empty (fully transparent) picture.
  // Never let such a picture replace a good one.
  function frameHasPixels(el){
    if(!probeCtx)return true;
    try{probeCtx.clearRect(0,0,4,4);probeCtx.drawImage(el,0,0,4,4);}catch(_e){return false;}
    try{
      const d=probeCtx.getImageData(0,0,4,4).data;
      for(let i=3;i<d.length;i+=4){if(d[i]>8)return true;}
      return false;
    }catch(_e){return true;} // read-back not permitted: the picture itself is still fine to draw
  }
  function cacheFrame(ov,fromCanvas,srcT){
    try{
      if(!fromCanvas||!fromCanvas.width||!fromCanvas.height)return;
      const last=frameCache[frameCache.length-1];
      if(last&&last.src===ov.src&&Math.abs(last.srcT-srcT)<0.06)return;
      const sc=Math.min(1,FRAME_CACHE_SIDE/Math.max(fromCanvas.width,fromCanvas.height));
      const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(fromCanvas.width*sc)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(fromCanvas.height*sc));
      const entry=frameCache.length>=FRAME_CACHE_MAX?frameCache.shift():{canvas:document.createElement('canvas')};
      if(entry.canvas.width!==w)entry.canvas.width=w;
      if(entry.canvas.height!==h)entry.canvas.height=h;
      const cx=entry.canvas.getContext('2d');if(!cx)return;
      cx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);cx.drawImage(fromCanvas,0,0,w,h);
      entry.src=ov.src;entry.srcT=srcT;
      frameCache.push(entry);
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  function pickCachedFrame(ov,expected){
    let best=null,bestD=Infinity;
    for(let i=frameCache.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const e=frameCache[i];if(e.src!==ov.src)continue;
      const d=Math.abs(e.srcT-expected);
      if(d<bestD){best=e;bestD=d;}
    }
    if(best)return best;
    // Another video clip's last picture is still better than an empty stage, but only while
    // that clip still exists in the project.
    const list=overlayList();
    for(let i=frameCache.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const e=frameCache[i];
      if(list.some(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&o.src===e.src))return e;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function makeHoldCanvas(ov,el){
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.className='ev-v138-scrub-hold-frame';
    canvas.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    el.__evScrubHoldCanvas=canvas;
    heldById.set(String(ov.id),{el,canvas});
    return canvas;
  }
  function dropHoldRecord(el,id,canvas){
    try{if(canvas)canvas.remove();}catch(_e){}
    if(el&&el.__evScrubHoldCanvas===canvas)delete el.__evScrubHoldCanvas;
    heldById.delete(String(id));
  }
  function drawHoldPixels(canvas,el){
    // The hold is only a mobile preview aid: cap it near 1280px / 2 megapixels rather
    // than allocating a full 4K backing store for every video while the finger is moving.
    const sourceW=el.videoWidth,sourceH=el.videoHeight;
    const scale=Math.min(1,1280/Math.max(sourceW,sourceH),Math.sqrt(2000000/Math.max(1,sourceW*sourceH)));
    const holdW=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceW*scale)),holdH=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceH*scale));
    if(canvas.width!==holdW||canvas.height!==holdH){canvas.width=holdW;canvas.height=holdH;}
    const cx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    if(!cx)return false;
    try{cx.clearRect(0,0,holdW,holdH);cx.drawImage(el,0,0,holdW,holdH);}catch(_e){return false;}
    return true;
  }
  function styleHold(canvas,ov,el,tSec,pixelsOnly){
    const layer=el.parentNode;
    if(!layer)return;
    if(!pixelsOnly){
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
      canvas.style.objectFit=cs.objectFit||'contain';
      canvas.style.objectPosition=cs.objectPosition||'center';
      canvas.style.transform=el.style.transform||cs.transform||'';
      canvas.style.transformOrigin=el.style.transformOrigin||cs.transformOrigin||'center center';
      canvas.style.filter=el.style.filter||cs.filter||'none';
      canvas.style.clipPath=el.style.clipPath||cs.clipPath||'none';
      canvas.style.borderRadius=el.style.borderRadius||cs.borderRadius||'0';
      // The video itself is parked at opacity 0 while this canvas stands in for it, so never
      // copy that 0 onto the canvas.
      const o=cs.opacity;
      canvas.style.opacity=(o===''||o==='0')?(canvas.style.opacity||'1'):o;
      if(cs.zIndex&&cs.zIndex!=='auto')canvas.style.zIndex=cs.zIndex;else canvas.style.removeProperty('z-index');
      canvas.style.background=cs.backgroundColor||'#08090c';
      canvas.dataset.evMaskOwnerId=String(ov.id);
      canvas.dataset.evMaskOwnerTime=String(Number(tSec)||0);
    }
    // Keep it immediately above its own video but below the next timeline overlay.
    if(canvas.parentNode!==layer||el.nextSibling!==canvas)layer.insertBefore(canvas,el.nextSibling);
    if(!pixelsOnly&&typeof window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle==='function')window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(canvas,ov,Number(tSec)||0,el,false);
  }
  function ensureHoldCanvas(ov,el,tSec,pixelsOnly){
    if(!ov||!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||!el.isConnected)return null;
    // Chroma key has its own transparent processed canvas. A raw-frame hold would bypass it.
    if(ov.chromaKey&&ov.chromaKey.enabled)return null;
    if(!el.parentNode)return null;
    let canvas=el.__evScrubHoldCanvas||null;
    const decodable=el.readyState>=2&&!el.seeking&&el.videoWidth>0&&el.videoHeight>0;
    if(decodable){
      // Only re-capture when the decoder is idle on a NEW picture, and only keep it when it
      // really contains something.
      const stamp=(Number(el.currentTime)||0).toFixed(3);
      const fresh=!!canvas&&canvas.dataset.evHoldReal==='1'&&canvas.dataset.evHoldStamp===stamp;
      if(!fresh&&frameHasPixels(el)){
        const created=!canvas;
        if(!canvas)canvas=makeHoldCanvas(ov,el);
        if(drawHoldPixels(canvas,el)){
          canvas.dataset.evHoldReal='1';canvas.dataset.evHoldStamp=stamp;
          cacheFrame(ov,canvas,Number(el.currentTime)||0);
        }else if(created){dropHoldRecord(el,ov.id,canvas);canvas=null;}
      }
    }
    if(!canvas)return null;
    styleHold(canvas,ov,el,tSec,!!pixelsOnly);
    return canvas;
  }
  // A clip that just became active has no decoded picture yet. Show the nearest cached picture
  // of the same source (or the most recent one) instead of an empty stage.
  function coveredByOutgoing(ov){
    const map=(window.renderOverlayMedia&&window.renderOverlayMedia.__evHeldOutgoing)||baseRender.__evHeldOutgoing||{};
    return Object.keys(map).some(k=>{
      const h=map[k];
      return !!h&&String(h.incomingId)===String(ov.id)&&!!(h.canvas||h.fallbackVideo);
    });
  }
  function seedHold(ov,el,tSec){
    if(!ov||!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||!el.isConnected||el.__evScrubHoldCanvas)return null;
    if(ov.chromaKey&&ov.chromaKey.enabled)return null;
    if(!frameCache.length||!el.parentNode)return null;
    const expected=typeof window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime==='function'?window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime(ov,tSec,el):0;
    const entry=pickCachedFrame(ov,expected);
    if(!entry)return null;
    const canvas=makeHoldCanvas(ov,el);
    canvas.width=entry.canvas.width;canvas.height=entry.canvas.height;
    const cx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    if(!cx){dropHoldRecord(el,ov.id,canvas);return null;}
    try{cx.drawImage(entry.canvas,0,0);}catch(_e){dropHoldRecord(el,ov.id,canvas);return null;}
    canvas.dataset.evHoldSeeded='1';
    styleHold(canvas,ov,el,tSec,false);
    return canvas;
  }
  function removeHold(el,id){
    if(!el)return;
    const canvas=el.__evScrubHoldCanvas;
    if(canvas){try{canvas.remove();}catch(_e){}delete el.__evScrubHoldCanvas;}
    delete el.__evHoldReadyAt;delete el.__evHoldPainted;
    // Normal video overlays are fully opaque; the core renderer will reapply the correct
    // visibility/opacity on its next pass. Restore immediately to avoid a one-frame blank.
    if(!overlayList().some(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id)&&o.chromaKey&&o.chromaKey.enabled))el.style.opacity='1';
    heldById.delete(String(id));
  }
  function finishHoldRelease(el,id){
    if(!el||!el.__evScrubHoldCanvas)return;
    if(window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing)return;
    if(el.seeking||el.readyState<2)return;
    removeHold(el,id);
  }
  // "Ready" only means the decoder reports the right time; on Android the surface can still be
  // blank for a frame. Keep the hold over the video until a frame has really been presented.
  function releaseHoldWhenPainted(el,ov){
    const now=performance.now();
    if(!el.__evHoldReadyAt){
      const token=now;
      el.__evHoldReadyAt=token;el.__evHoldPainted=false;
      const settle=()=>{
        if(el.__evHoldReadyAt!==token)return;
        el.__evHoldPainted=true;
        finishHoldRelease(el,ov.id);
      };
      if(typeof el.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function'){try{el.requestVideoFrameCallback(settle);}catch(_e){}}
      setTimeout(settle,HOLD_RELEASE_GRACE_MS);
    }
    if(el.__evHoldPainted||now-el.__evHoldReadyAt>=HOLD_RELEASE_GRACE_MS)finishHoldRelease(el,ov.id);
  }
  function syncReleasedVideo(ov,el,t){
    if(!ov||!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing||(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying))return;
    const releaseId=String(window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubReleaseId||'');
    if(!releaseId||el.dataset.evScrubFinalSyncedId===releaseId)return;
    if(el.seeking||el.readyState<1)return;
    const expected=typeof window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime==='function'
      ?window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime(ov,t,el)
      :Math.max(0,(Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+(Number(t)-Number(ov.trimStart||0))*Math.max(.05,Number(ov.speed)||1));
    const delta=Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-expected);
    if(delta<=0.035){el.dataset.evScrubFinalSyncedId=releaseId;delete el.dataset.evScrubFinalRequestedId;return;}
    // Do not launch duplicate seeks while the previous decoder request is outstanding.
    if(el.dataset.evScrubFinalRequestedId===releaseId)return;
    el.dataset.evScrubFinalRequestedId=releaseId;
    const now=performance.now();
    el.dataset.evSeekRequestAt=String(now);
    el.dataset.evPlaySeekAt=String(now);
    try{el.currentTime=expected;}catch(_e){delete el.dataset.evScrubFinalRequestedId;}
  }

  const wrappedRender=function(tSec){
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(tSec))?Number(tSec):currentTimeSec();
    const active=activeVideosAt(t);
    const scrubbing=!!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing;

    // Snapshot the last decoded picture BEFORE the core renderer assigns another currentTime.
    // Updating only when the decoder is not seeking ensures a valid image survives rapid finger
    // movement even when Android's video surface temporarily blanks during a seek.
    if(scrubbing){
      active.forEach(ov=>{
        const el=getVideo(ov.id);
        if(el)ensureHoldCanvas(ov,el,t,false);
      });
    }

    const result=baseRender.apply(this,arguments);
    const liveIds=new Set(active.map(o=>String(o.id)));
    const releasedRecently=performance.now()-(Number(window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubReleaseAt)||0)<1500;

    active.forEach(ov=>{
      const el=getVideo(ov.id);
      if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
      if(!scrubbing)syncReleasedVideo(ov,el,t);
      let ready=false;
      try{ready=typeof window.evOverlayVideoFrameIsPresentable==='function'&&window.evOverlayVideoFrameIsPresentable(ov,el,t);}catch(_e){}
      let hold=el.__evScrubHoldCanvas;
      // No picture of its own yet (clip just entered while dragging): borrow the nearest cached one.
      if(!hold&&!ready&&(scrubbing||releasedRecently)&&!coveredByOutgoing(ov))hold=seedHold(ov,el,t);
      if(hold&&!hold.isConnected&&el.parentNode)el.parentNode.insertBefore(hold,el.nextSibling);
      if(!hold||!hold.isConnected)return;
      if(ready&&!el.seeking){releaseHoldWhenPainted(el,ov);return;}
      delete el.__evHoldReadyAt;delete el.__evHoldPainted;
      // The retained canvas stays visible while the video decoder catches up. Set opacity,
      // rather than visibility, on the video so it cannot paint a black seek surface over it.
      hold.style.opacity=(ov.chromaKey&&ov.chromaKey.enabled)?'0':(getComputedStyle(el).opacity==='0'?'1':getComputedStyle(el).opacity||'1');
      el.style.opacity='0';
      const layer=el.parentNode;
      if(layer&&(hold.parentNode!==layer||el.nextSibling!==hold))layer.insertBefore(hold,el.nextSibling);
    });

    // The core renderer removes outgoing <video> nodes at clip boundaries. Its own outgoing
    // freeze canvas has already copied our retained frame, so remove the temporary scrub copy.
    for(const [id,entry] of Array.from(heldById.entries())){
      if(liveIds.has(id))continue;
      // v207: contiguous split clips hand their <video> decoder to the next clip (the element is
      // re-keyed to the new clip id). Its retained picture must travel with it - deleting it here
      // blanked the stage every time a drag crossed a split point.
      const handedTo=active.find(o=>entry.el&&getVideo(o.id)===entry.el&&entry.el.__evScrubHoldCanvas===entry.canvas);
      if(handedTo){
        heldById.delete(id);
        if(!heldById.has(String(handedTo.id)))heldById.set(String(handedTo.id),entry);
        continue;
      }
      const outgoing=window.renderOverlayMedia&&window.renderOverlayMedia.__evHeldOutgoing&&window.renderOverlayMedia.__evHeldOutgoing[id];
      // If the core compositor had to retain the outgoing video element as a fallback, keep
      // our last-good canvas beside it until that outgoing hold is released.
      if(outgoing&&entry.el&&(outgoing.fallbackVideo===entry.el||outgoing.canvas===entry.canvas))continue;
      if(entry.canvas){try{entry.canvas.remove();}catch(_e){}}
      if(entry.el){delete entry.el.__evScrubHoldCanvas;delete entry.el.__evHoldReadyAt;delete entry.el.__evHoldPainted;if(entry.el.isConnected)entry.el.style.opacity='1';}
      heldById.delete(id);
    }
    return result;
  };
  wrappedRender.__ethvelleV138StableScrub=true;
  window.renderOverlayMedia=wrappedRender;

  // A scrub may finish while an earlier, rate-limited seek is still decoding. When that seek
  // completes, run one render pass: syncReleasedVideo then issues the exact final seek, and the
  // following seeked event releases the retained image as soon as the requested frame is ready.
  const layer=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer');
  if(layer&&!layer.dataset.evV138SeekedSync){
    layer.dataset.evV138SeekedSync='1';
    layer.addEventListener('seeked',e=>{
      const el=e.target;
      if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||el.dataset.evWarmElement==='1')return;
      // v207: a seek just landed during a drag - refresh the retained picture right now, not
      // at the next throttled render, so the freshest decoded frame is never lost.
      if(window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing){
        try{
          const ov=overlayForVideo(el),t=currentTimeSec();
          if(ov&&!ov.reversePlayback&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0))ensureHoldCanvas(ov,el,t,true);
        }catch(_e){}
      }
      if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing&&!(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)&&window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubReleaseId){
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}});
      }
    },true);
  }
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle v139: gapless audio preloading, slim waveforms, and deterministic redo commits. -->
<script id="ethvelle-v139-history-navigation-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV139HistoryNavigationInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV139HistoryNavigationInstalled=true;
  const oldUndo=window.undoHistory;
  const oldRedo=window.redoHistory;
  const oldReset=window.resetHistory;
  if(typeof oldUndo==='function'){
    window.undoHistory=function(){
      if(typeof window.__ethvelleV139FlushHistory==='function')window.__ethvelleV139FlushHistory();
      const result=oldUndo.apply(this,arguments);
      if(typeof window.updateHistoryButtons==='function')window.updateHistoryButtons();
      return result;
    };
  }
  if(typeof oldRedo==='function'){
    window.redoHistory=function(){
      if(typeof window.__ethvelleV139FlushHistory==='function')window.__ethvelleV139FlushHistory();
      const result=oldRedo.apply(this,arguments);
      if(typeof window.updateHistoryButtons==='function')window.updateHistoryButtons();
      return result;
    };
  }
  if(typeof oldReset==='function'){
    window.resetHistory=function(){
      if(typeof window.__ethvelleV139ResetHistoryTransaction==='function')window.__ethvelleV139ResetHistoryTransaction();
      const result=oldReset.apply(this,arguments);
      if(typeof window.updateHistoryButtons==='function')window.updateHistoryButtons();
      return result;
    };
  }
  // If a project switch/reset occurs while no editor pointer is down, keep the new baseline
  // synchronized with the visible button state.
  if(typeof window.updateHistoryButtons==='function')window.updateHistoryButtons();
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v140: gesture-unlocked overlay audio, normalized compact history, dense waveforms. -->
<script id="ethvelle-v140-fixes">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV140FixesInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV140FixesInstalled=true;

  /* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
     Shared helpers
     -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  const V140_WAVE_CACHE=window.__ethvelleV140WaveCache||(window.__ethvelleV140WaveCache=new Map());
  const V140_HISTORY_PAYLOADS=window.__ethvelleV140HistoryPayloads||(window.__ethvelleV140HistoryPayloads=new Map());
  const V140_PLAY_WARN_AT=new Map();
  let V140_LAST_HISTORY_NAV_AT=0;
  let V140_LAST_POINTER_DRAG=false;

  function v140LiveOverlay(id,type){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return null;
    return overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id)&&(!type||o.type===type))||null;
  }
  function v140LiveProxy(ov){
    if(!ov||ov.id==null)return ov;
    const id=String(ov.id),fallback=ov;
    try{
      return new Proxy(fallback,{
        get(target,key){const live=v140LiveOverlay(id)||target;return Reflect.get(live,key,live);},
        set(target,key,value){const live=v140LiveOverlay(id)||target;return Reflect.set(live,key,value,live);},
        has(target,key){const live=v140LiveOverlay(id)||target;return Reflect.has(live,key);}
      });
    }catch(_e){
      const proxy={};
      const props=new Set(Object.keys(fallback).concat(['id','src','type','trimStart','trimEnd','sourceOffset','sourceDuration','speed','reversePlayback','volume','keyframes','chromaKey']));
      props.forEach(key=>{try{Object.defineProperty(proxy,key,{enumerable:true,configurable:true,get(){const live=v140LiveOverlay(id)||fallback;return live[key];},set(value){const live=v140LiveOverlay(id)||fallback;live[key]=value;}});}catch(_e){}});
      return proxy;
    }
  }
  function v140ShowUnlockToast(){
    if(window.__ethvelleV140UnlockToastShown)return;
    window.__ethvelleV140UnlockToastShown=true;
    try{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Tap Play once to enable sound',{duration:2400});}catch(_e){}
  }
  function v140ReportPlayError(err,kind,id){
    const name=String(err&&err.name||'Error');
    const key=String(kind||'media')+':'+String(id||'')+':'+name;
    const now=Date.now(),last=V140_PLAY_WARN_AT.get(key)||0;
    if(now-last>900){
      V140_PLAY_WARN_AT.set(key,now);
      try{console.warn('Ethvelle '+kind+' play() rejected: '+name,err);}catch(_e){}
    }
    if(name==='NotAllowedError')v140ShowUnlockToast();
  }
  function v140InstrumentPlay(el,kind,id){
    if(!el||el.__evV140PlayInstrumented)return;
    try{
      const nativePlay=el.play.bind(el);
      el.play=function(){
        let result;
        try{result=nativePlay();}
        catch(err){v140ReportPlayError(err,kind,id);throw err;}
        if(result&&typeof result.then==='function')result.catch(err=>v140ReportPlayError(err,kind,id));
        return result;
      };
      el.__evV140PlayInstrumented=true;
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle could not instrument '+kind+' play():',err);}
  }
  function v140ResumeAudioContext(){
    try{
      const ctx=typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function'?ensureAudioOverlayContext():null;
      if(ctx&&ctx.state==='suspended'){
        const promise=ctx.resume();
        if(promise&&typeof promise.catch==='function')promise.catch(err=>console.warn('Ethvelle AudioContext resume rejected:',err&&err.name||'Error',err));
      }
      return ctx;
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle AudioContext resume failed:',err&&err.name||'Error',err);return null;}
  }

  /* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
     BUG 1 — prime every per-clip media element in the actual Play gesture.
     Existing per-id elements are intentionally retained because each owns its own
     MediaElementSource and DSP graph (voice, EQ, noise gate, fades and pan).
     -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  const V140_VIDEO_NODES=window.__ethvelleV140VideoNodes||(window.__ethvelleV140VideoNodes=new WeakMap());
  function v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,id){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return null;
    let nodes=V140_VIDEO_NODES.get(el)||el.__evV140AudioNodes||null;
    if(nodes)return nodes;
    const ctx=v140ResumeAudioContext();
    if(!ctx)return null;
    try{
      const source=ctx.createMediaElementSource(el),gain=ctx.createGain();
      source.connect(gain);gain.connect(ctx.destination);gain.gain.value=0;
      nodes={ctx,source,gain,id:String(id||el.dataset.evV72OverlayId||'')};
      V140_VIDEO_NODES.set(el,nodes);el.__evV140AudioNodes=nodes;
      return nodes;
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('Ethvelle video audio gain setup failed:',err&&err.name||'Error',err);
      return null;
    }
  }
  function v140SetVideoGain(el,value,fadeSeconds){
    const nodes=el&&(V140_VIDEO_NODES.get(el)||el.__evV140AudioNodes);
    const v=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Number(value)||0));
    if(nodes&&nodes.gain){
      try{
        const param=nodes.gain.gain,ctx=nodes.ctx;
        param.cancelScheduledValues(ctx.currentTime);
        if(fadeSeconds>0){param.setValueAtTime(param.value,ctx.currentTime);param.linearRampToValueAtTime(v,ctx.currentTime+fadeSeconds);}
        else param.setValueAtTime(v,ctx.currentTime);
      }catch(_e){try{nodes.gain.gain.value=v;}catch(__e){}}
      try{el.volume=1;el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');}catch(_e){}
    }else{
      try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=Math.min(1,v);}catch(_e){}
    }
    return !!nodes;
  }
  function v140StopVideo(el,pauseIt){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
    if(el.__evGainRampRaf){try{cancelAnimationFrame(el.__evGainRampRaf);}catch(_e){}el.__evGainRampRaf=0;}
    delete el.dataset.evGainRampActive;delete el.dataset.evGainRampDone;delete el.dataset.evStablePlayingSince;delete el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen;
    v140SetVideoGain(el,0,0);
    if(pauseIt!==false&&!el.paused){try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}}
  }
  function v140MuteOtherVideos(keepEl){
    // v279: overlapping video layers must ALL be heard (doubled sound), so nothing is muted here.
    return;
    if(typeof overlayMediaEls==='undefined')return;
    Object.keys(overlayMediaEls).forEach(id=>{
      const other=overlayMediaEls[id];
      if(!other||other===keepEl||other.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
      if(other.__evGainRampRaf){try{cancelAnimationFrame(other.__evGainRampRaf);}catch(_e){}other.__evGainRampRaf=0;}
      delete other.dataset.evGainRampActive;
      v140SetVideoGain(other,0,0);
    });
  }
  function v140VideoPreferred(id,time){
    // v279: every active forward-playing video layer is audible, not just the topmost one, so two
    // overlapping copies of a clip are heard together (doubled) instead of one being auto-muted.
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return false;
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const ov=overlays.find(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&String(o.id)===String(id));
    return !!(ov&&!ov.reversePlayback&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0));
  }
  function v140RampVideoAudible(el,ov){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return false;
    const id=String((ov&&ov.id)||el.dataset.evV72OverlayId||'');
    const live=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video');
    if(!live)return false;
    const t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const active=t>=Number(live.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(live.trimEnd||0);
    if(el.dataset.evV140Priming==='1'&&!active){v140SetVideoGain(el,0,0);return true;}
    const aligned=isPlaying&&!live.reversePlayback&&active&&el.isConnected&&el.readyState>=2&&!el.seeking&&
      el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen==='1'&&!(evVideoBufferStall&&String(evVideoBufferStall.id)===id);
    // Keep audio attached to a live video element during ordinary mobile decoder drift.
    // The visual compositor already gates presentation separately; pausing the whole video
    // here left playback visibly running after the browser's mute flag had been restored.
    if(!aligned){v140StopVideo(el,true);return false;}
    if(!v140VideoPreferred(id,t)){
      v140SetVideoGain(el,0,0);return false;
    }
    v140MuteOtherVideos(el);
    if(el.__evGainRampRaf)return true;
    const rawTarget=el.dataset.evTargetVolume==null?(live.volume==null?1:Number(live.volume)):Number(el.dataset.evTargetVolume);
    const target=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number.isFinite(rawTarget)?rawTarget:1));
    const nodes=v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,id);
    if(el.dataset.evGainRampDone==='1'){
      v140SetVideoGain(el,target,0);return true;
    }
    const start=performance.now();
    if(nodes)v140SetVideoGain(el,0,0);
    else{try{el.volume=0;el.muted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');}catch(_e){}}
    el.dataset.evGainRampActive='1';
    const step=()=>{
      el.__evGainRampRaf=0;
      const now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      const current=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video');
      if(!current||!isPlaying||now<Number(current.trimStart||0)||now>=Number(current.trimEnd||0)||
         (evVideoBufferStall&&String(evVideoBufferStall.id)===id)||el.seeking||!el.isConnected){v140StopVideo(el,true);return;}
      const amount=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(performance.now()-start)/110));
      v140SetVideoGain(el,target*amount,0);
      if(amount<1)el.__evGainRampRaf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
      else{delete el.dataset.evGainRampActive;el.dataset.evGainRampDone='1';v140SetVideoGain(el,target,0);}
    };
    el.__evGainRampRaf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    return true;
  }
  function v140SafePlayVideo(el,ov){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return Promise.resolve(false);
    const id=String((ov&&ov.id)||el.dataset.evV72OverlayId||'');
    const live=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video');
    if(!live)return Promise.resolve(false);
    const now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const active=now>=Number(live.trimStart||0)&&now<Number(live.trimEnd||0);
    if(el.dataset.evV140Priming==='1'&&!active){v140SetVideoGain(el,0,0);return Promise.resolve(false);}
    if(live.reversePlayback||!active||(evVideoBufferStall&&String(evVideoBufferStall.id)===id)){
      v140StopVideo(el,true);return Promise.resolve(false);
    }
    if(el.seeking||el.readyState<2)return Promise.resolve(!el.paused);
    const expected=evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime(live,now,el);
    const drift=Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-expected);
    const seekAt=Number(el.dataset.evPlaySeekAt)||0;
    const justSeeked=seekAt>0&&performance.now()-seekAt<1500;
    const tolerance=(!el.paused||justSeeked)?EV_VIDEO_ALIGN_TOL_PLAYING:EV_VIDEO_ALIGN_TOL_START;
    if(drift>tolerance){
      v140StopVideo(el,true);
      if(!el.seeking&&el.readyState>=1){
        const latency=Math.max(0,Math.min(1200,Number(el.dataset.evSeekLatencyMs)||0));
        const speed=Math.max(.05,Number(live.speed)||1);
        const maxT=Math.max(.05,(Number(live.sourceDuration)||Number(el.duration)||expected+.05)-.02);
        const target=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxT,expected+(latency/1000)*speed));
        el.dataset.evPreciseSyncTarget=String(target);el.dataset.evSeekRequestAt=String(performance.now());
        try{el.currentTime=target;}catch(_e){delete el.dataset.evPreciseSyncTarget;}
      }
      return Promise.resolve(false);
    }
    delete el.dataset.evPreciseSyncTarget;
    v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,id);v140InstrumentPlay(el,'video',id);
    try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=1;}catch(_e){}
    if(!el.paused){if(isPlaying&&el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen==='1')v140RampVideoAudible(el,live);return Promise.resolve(true);}
    if(el.dataset.evV140PlayPending==='1')return el.__evV140PrimePromise||Promise.resolve(false);
    el.dataset.evV140PlayPending='1';
    let nativePromise;
    try{nativePromise=el.play();}catch(err){delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;v140ReportPlayError(err,'video',id);return Promise.resolve(false);}
    const attempt=Promise.resolve(nativePromise).then(()=>{
      delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;
      const current=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video'),t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      if(current&&isPlaying&&t>=Number(current.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(current.trimEnd||0))v140RampVideoAudible(el,current);
      else v140StopVideo(el,true);
      return true;
    },err=>{delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;v140ReportPlayError(err,'video',id);return false;});
    el.__evV140PrimePromise=attempt;
    return attempt;
  }

  const oldExpected=window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime;
  if(typeof oldExpected==='function')window.evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime=function(ov,time,el){return oldExpected.call(this,v140LiveProxy(ov),time,el);};
  const oldPresentable=window.evOverlayVideoFrameIsPresentable;
  if(typeof oldPresentable==='function')window.evOverlayVideoFrameIsPresentable=function(ov,el,time){return oldPresentable.call(this,v140LiveProxy(ov),el,time);};
  const oldActive=window.evOverlayVideoIsActiveAt;
  if(typeof oldActive==='function')window.evOverlayVideoIsActiveAt=function(ov,time){return oldActive.call(this,v140LiveProxy(ov),time);};
  window.evStopOverlayVideo=v140StopVideo;
  window.evMuteOtherOverlayVideos=v140MuteOtherVideos;
  window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible=v140RampVideoAudible;
  window.evSafePlayVideo=v140SafePlayVideo;
  window.evSafePlayOverlayVideo=v140SafePlayVideo;
  const oldWatch=window.evWatchIncomingOverlayVideo;
  if(typeof oldWatch==='function')window.evWatchIncomingOverlayVideo=function(ov,el){return oldWatch.call(this,v140LiveProxy(ov),el);};

  const oldSetupVideo=window.setupOverlayVideoEl;
  if(typeof oldSetupVideo==='function'){
    window.setupOverlayVideoEl=function(el,ov){
      const liveProxy=v140LiveProxy(ov),id=String(ov&&ov.id||'');
      const result=oldSetupVideo.call(this,el,liveProxy);
      v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,id);v140InstrumentPlay(el,'video',id);
      try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=1;}catch(_e){}
      if(!el.__evV140PauseWrapped){
        try{
          const nativePause=el.pause.bind(el);
          el.pause=function(){
            const live=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video'),t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
            const active=!!live&&t>=Number(live.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(live.trimEnd||0);
            if(el.dataset.evV140Priming==='1'&&isPlaying&&!active&&el.dataset.evV140AllowPrimePause!=='1')return;
            return nativePause();
          };
          el.__evV140PauseWrapped=true;
        }catch(_e){}
      }
      return result;
    };
  }

  const oldReleaseVideo=window.releaseOverlayVideoEl;
  if(typeof oldReleaseVideo==='function'){
    window.releaseOverlayVideoEl=function(el){
      // v278 FIX (audio dies after split + extend/trim/enhance): a <video> element can be bound to a
      // MediaElementSourceNode only ONCE in its lifetime, and from then on ALL of its sound flows through
      // that node. Disconnecting the node (what this used to do) left the pooled element permanently
      // silent, and the next createMediaElementSource() on it threw "already connected previously".
      // The source->gain->destination chain therefore stays wired; releasing just mutes it, and the
      // same nodes are reused when the pooled element is handed to another clip.
      const nodes=el&&(V140_VIDEO_NODES.get(el)||el.__evV140AudioNodes);
      if(nodes){
        try{
          const param=nodes.gain.gain,at=nodes.ctx.currentTime;
          param.cancelScheduledValues(at);param.setValueAtTime(0,at);
        }catch(_e){try{nodes.gain.gain.value=0;}catch(__e){}}
        try{V140_VIDEO_NODES.set(el,nodes);el.__evV140AudioNodes=nodes;}catch(_e){}
      }
      // BUGFIX v150: this used to re-park every released decoder forever ("v140-loop-pool")
      // instead of actually releasing it, with no cap — on a timeline with many clips that
      // meant dozens of live <video> decoders accumulating over a single playback pass. The
      // bounded warm pool now prepares only adjacent clips that follow the current playhead;
      // there is no loop-start decoder to preserve because playback stops at the content end.
      if(window.__evVideoDebug)try{console.log('[evVideo] release',el&&el.dataset&&el.dataset.evV72OverlayId);}catch(_e){}
      return oldReleaseVideo.apply(this,arguments);
    };
  }

  const oldWarmVideos=window.warmUpcomingOverlayVideos;
  if(typeof oldWarmVideos==='function'){
    window.warmUpcomingOverlayVideos=function(t){
      // Keep the bounded adjacent-clip warm pool active for new clips and after scrubs.
      // Playback stops at the project-content end, so no loop-wrap decoder is prepared.
      const result=oldWarmVideos.apply(this,arguments);
      Object.keys(overlayWarmEls).forEach(id=>{
        const el=overlayWarmEls[id];if(!el)return;
        v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,id);v140InstrumentPlay(el,'video',id);
        try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=1;}catch(_e){}
      });
      return result;
    };
  }

  // Render first, while still inside Play's user-activation stack, so the current video
  // receives its normal compositor element before the gesture-prime loop begins.
  function v140PrepareMediaForGesture(){
    const time=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    window.__ethvelleV140PreprimedSession=true;
    v140ResumeAudioContext();
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(time);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle media gesture preparation:',err);}
    const audios=[];
    (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='audio'&&o.src&&o.id).forEach(ov=>{
      let el=null;
      try{el=evAudioOverlayEnsure(ov);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle audio element preparation:',err);}
      if(!el)return;
      const id=String(ov.id);v140InstrumentPlay(el,'audio',id);
      const nodes=audioOverlayNodes&&audioOverlayNodes[id];
      if(nodes&&nodes.gain&&audioOverlayAudioCtx){try{nodes.gain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(audioOverlayAudioCtx.currentTime);nodes.gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0,audioOverlayAudioCtx.currentTime);}catch(_e){nodes.gain.gain.value=0;}}
      const cfg=typeof evAudioOverlayRate==='function'?evAudioOverlayRate(ov):{rate:Math.max(.05,Number(ov.speed)||1)};
      const active=time>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&time<Number(ov.trimEnd||0);
      const target=active?evAudioOverlayLocalTime(ov,time,el,cfg.rate):Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0);
      if(el.readyState>=1&&!el.seeking&&Math.abs((Number(el.currentTime)||0)-target)>.04){try{el.currentTime=target;}catch(_e){}}
      el.dataset.evAudioNeedsSync='1';el.dataset.evV140Priming='1';
      try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=1;}catch(_e){}
      audios.push({id,el});
    });

    const videos=[],layer=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer');
    // BUGFIX v150 (stage blanks + audio crackle at cuts): this used to prime EVERY video
    // clip in the whole project inside the Play gesture — a decoder, a MediaElementSource
    // and a play() call for clips minutes away on the timeline. On a phone that meant many
    // simultaneous decoders competing for the same hardware decode/audio budget, which is
    // what produced the blank-canvas stalls and audio dropouts at clip cuts. Only the
    // clip(s) actually active the instant Play is pressed need a decoder unlocked inside
    // this trusted gesture; everything else is already pre-warmed, bounded to EV_WARM_MAX
    // (2) next/previous decoders, by warmUpcomingOverlayVideos as the timeline approaches it.
    if(window.__evVideoDebug)try{console.log('[evVideo] gesture-prime scan at',time);}catch(_e){}
    (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(o)&&o.id&&!o.reversePlayback&&
      time>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&time<Number(o.trimEnd||0)).forEach(ov=>{
      const id=String(ov.id);let el=overlayMediaEls[id]||overlayWarmEls[id]||null;
      const effectiveSrc=window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(ov);
      if(el&&String(el.dataset.evWarmSrc||el.getAttribute('src')||el.src||'')!==effectiveSrc){
        if(overlayWarmEls[id]===el)delete overlayWarmEls[id];
        try{const nodes=V140_VIDEO_NODES.get(el)||el.__evV140AudioNodes;if(nodes){const at=nodes.ctx.currentTime;nodes.gain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(at);nodes.gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0,at);}}catch(_e){}
        try{oldReleaseVideo&&oldReleaseVideo(el);}catch(_e){}
        if(overlayMediaEls[id]===el)delete overlayMediaEls[id];
        el=null;
      }
      if(!el){
        if(!layer)return;
        el=document.createElement('video');
        el.dataset.evWarmElement='1';el.dataset.evWarmSrc=effectiveSrc;
        el.dataset.evWarmTarget=String(Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0));
        el.dataset.evWarmRole='v140-gesture-prime';
        el.style.visibility='hidden';el.style.opacity='0';el.style.pointerEvents='none';
        layer.appendChild(el);
        try{setupOverlayVideoEl(el,v140LiveProxy(ov));}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle video element preparation:',err);}
        overlayWarmEls[id]=el;
      }
      if(!el.isConnected&&layer)layer.appendChild(el);
      el.dataset.evWarmSrc=effectiveSrc;
      v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,id);v140InstrumentPlay(el,'video',id);
      v140SetVideoGain(el,0,0);
      try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=1;}catch(_e){}
      el.dataset.evV140Priming='1';
      videos.push({id,el});
    });
    return {audios,videos};
  }
  function v140PrimeOneAudio(entry){
    const el=entry.el,id=entry.id,live=v140LiveOverlay(id,'audio');if(!el||!live)return;
    el.dataset.evAudioPlayPending='1';
    let p;
    try{p=el.play();}catch(err){delete el.dataset.evAudioPlayPending;v140ReportPlayError(err,'audio',id);return;}
    if(p&&typeof p.then==='function'){
      p.then(()=>{
        delete el.dataset.evAudioPlayPending;delete el.dataset.evV140Priming;
        const current=v140LiveOverlay(id,'audio'),t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        if(!current||!isPlaying||t<Number(current.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(current.trimEnd||0)){
          try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}
          if(current)el.dataset.evAudioNeedsSync='1';
        }
      },()=>{delete el.dataset.evAudioPlayPending;delete el.dataset.evV140Priming;});
    }else{delete el.dataset.evAudioPlayPending;delete el.dataset.evV140Priming;}
  }
  function v140PrimeOneVideo(entry){
    const el=entry.el,id=entry.id,live=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video');if(!el||!live)return;
    el.dataset.evV140PlayPending='1';
    let p;
    try{p=el.play();}catch(err){delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;v140ReportPlayError(err,'video',id);return;}
    if(p&&typeof p.then==='function'){
      p.then(()=>{
        delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;delete el.dataset.evV140Priming;
        const current=v140LiveOverlay(id,'video'),t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        if(current&&isPlaying&&t>=Number(current.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(current.trimEnd||0))v140RampVideoAudible(el,current);
        else{
          el.dataset.evV140AllowPrimePause='1';try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}delete el.dataset.evV140AllowPrimePause;
        }
      },()=>{delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;delete el.dataset.evV140Priming;});
    }else{
      delete el.dataset.evV140PlayPending;delete el.dataset.evV140Priming;
      const t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      if(!(isPlaying&&t>=Number(live.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(live.trimEnd||0))){el.dataset.evV140AllowPrimePause='1';try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}delete el.dataset.evV140AllowPrimePause;}
    }
  }

  const oldStartPlayback=window.startPlayback;
  if(typeof oldStartPlayback==='function'&&!oldStartPlayback.__ethvelleV140GesturePrime){
    const wrappedStart=function(){
      if(isPlaying)return oldStartPlayback.apply(this,arguments);
      const prepared=v140PrepareMediaForGesture();
      const result=oldStartPlayback.apply(this,arguments);
      // startPlayback sets isPlaying synchronously; these play() calls therefore still run
      // inside the same trusted Play pointer/click event, before its activation expires.
      if(isPlaying&&prepared){prepared.audios.forEach(v140PrimeOneAudio);prepared.videos.forEach(v140PrimeOneVideo);}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedStart.__ethvelleV140GesturePrime=true;window.startPlayback=wrappedStart;
  }
  const oldTogglePlayback=window.togglePlayback;
  if(typeof oldTogglePlayback==='function'&&!oldTogglePlayback.__ethvelleV140Toggle){
    const wrappedToggle=function(){return oldTogglePlayback.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedToggle.__ethvelleV140Toggle=true;window.togglePlayback=wrappedToggle;
  }

  // The video element keeps volume at 1. The per-video GainNode is the sole live volume
  // control, so later clips do not depend on changing `muted` after their load event.
  const oldRenderVideo=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderVideo==='function'&&!oldRenderVideo.__ethvelleV140Volume){
    const wrappedRenderVideo=function(t){
      const result=oldRenderVideo.apply(this,arguments);
      (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
        if(!ov||ov.type!=='video')return;
        const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
        v140EnsureVideoAudioNodes(el,ov.id);
        try{el.muted=false;el.defaultMuted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=1;}catch(_e){}
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRenderVideo.__ethvelleV140Volume=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrappedRenderVideo;
  }

  // Every user pointerdown is also an immediate retry opportunity after NotAllowedError.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(){
    if(!isPlaying)return;
    // A foreground/background transition can suspend Web Audio again on mobile. A real
    // user pointer gesture is the only reliable place to request another resume.
    v140ResumeAudioContext();
    const t=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
      if(!ov)return;
      if(ov.type==='audio'&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0)){
        const el=audioOverlayEls&&audioOverlayEls[String(ov.id)];if(el&&el.paused&&typeof evAudioOverlayStart==='function')evAudioOverlayStart(ov,el,t);
      }else if(ov.type==='video'&&!ov.reversePlayback&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0)){
        const el=(overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)])||(overlayWarmEls&&overlayWarmEls[String(ov.id)]);
        if(el&&el.paused)v140SafePlayVideo(el,ov);
      }
    });
  },true);

  /* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
     BUG 2 — normalized signatures + compact payloads, waveform/thumb side cache.
     `pushHistory()` in the original and v133 transaction wrapper both call the global
     snapshotState binding. Returning a stable signature here protects the redo branch
     even when v133 schedules a commit for a scrub/selection/play event.
     -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  function v140CacheOverlayAssets(list){
    (Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).forEach(ov=>{
      if(!ov||ov.id==null||(!ov.peaks&&!ov.thumbs))return;
      const key=String(ov.id)+'|'+String(ov.src||'');
      const prior=V140_WAVE_CACHE.get(key)||{};
      if(ov.peaks&&ov.peaks.length)prior.peaks=Array.from(ov.peaks,p=>Array.isArray(p)?[Number(p[0])||0,Number(p[1])||0]:p);
      if(ov.thumbs&&ov.thumbs.length)prior.thumbs=Array.from(ov.thumbs);
      V140_WAVE_CACHE.delete(key);V140_WAVE_CACHE.set(key,prior);
    });
    while(V140_WAVE_CACHE.size>96)V140_WAVE_CACHE.delete(V140_WAVE_CACHE.keys().next().value);
  }
  function v140StripHistoryFields(value,mode,inCharacters){
    if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(v=>v140StripHistoryFields(v,mode,inCharacters));
    if(!value||typeof value!=='object')return value;
    const out={};
    Object.keys(value).sort().forEach(key=>{
      if(key.startsWith('__')||key==='peaks'||key==='thumbs')return;
      if(['currentAbsoluteTimeSec','playheadTimeSec','playhead','currentTime','frame','currentFrame','runtimeFrame'].includes(key))return;
      if(mode==='signature'&&inCharacters&&['bones','baseAngles','modelTransform','restTransform','hipRestY'].includes(key))return;
      const childInCharacters=inCharacters||(key==='characters');
      out[key]=v140StripHistoryFields(value[key],mode,childInCharacters);
    });
    return out;
  }
  function v140StableStringify(value){
    if(Array.isArray(value))return '['+value.map(v140StableStringify).join(',')+']';
    if(value&&typeof value==='object'){
      return '{'+Object.keys(value).sort().map(k=>JSON.stringify(k)+':'+v140StableStringify(value[k])).join(',')+'}';
    }
    return JSON.stringify(value);
  }
  const oldSnapshotState=window.snapshotState;
  if(typeof oldSnapshotState==='function'&&!oldSnapshotState.__ethvelleV140Normalized){
    const wrappedSnapshot=function(){
      // Match the original snapshot's project fields, but never stringify the media caches.
      // This avoids allocating a huge temporary JSON string on every history commit.
      const full={currentAnimKey,activeClip,splitStickmanSegments,timelineDuration,bgDuration,
        characters,activeCharIdx,bgTransform,currentBg,backgrounds,overlays};
      v140CacheOverlayAssets(full.overlays);
      const payload=v140StripHistoryFields(full,'payload',false);
      const normalized=v140StripHistoryFields(full,'signature',false);
      const signature='__ethvelle_v140__'+v140StableStringify(normalized);
      V140_HISTORY_PAYLOADS.set(signature,JSON.stringify(payload));
      if(typeof historyStack!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(historyStack)){
        const keep=new Set(historyStack);keep.add(signature);
        Array.from(V140_HISTORY_PAYLOADS.keys()).forEach(key=>{if(!keep.has(key))V140_HISTORY_PAYLOADS.delete(key);});
      }
      return signature;
    };
    wrappedSnapshot.__ethvelleV140Normalized=true;window.snapshotState=wrappedSnapshot;
  }

  const oldApplySnapshot=window.applySnapshot;
  if(typeof oldApplySnapshot==='function'&&!oldApplySnapshot.__ethvelleV140Rehydrate){
    const wrappedApply=function(snap){
      let expanded=String(snap||'');
      const payload=V140_HISTORY_PAYLOADS.get(expanded);
      if(payload)expanded=payload;
      try{
        const data=JSON.parse(expanded);
        v140CacheOverlayAssets(data.overlays);
        (Array.isArray(data.overlays)?data.overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
          if(!ov||ov.id==null)return;
          const cached=V140_WAVE_CACHE.get(String(ov.id)+'|'+String(ov.src||''));
          if(cached){if(!ov.peaks&&cached.peaks)ov.peaks=Array.from(cached.peaks,p=>Array.isArray(p)?[p[0],p[1]]:p);if(!ov.thumbs&&cached.thumbs)ov.thumbs=Array.from(cached.thumbs);}
        });
        expanded=JSON.stringify(data);
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle history snapshot rehydrate:',err);}
      const result=oldApplySnapshot.call(this,expanded);
      const targetIndex=typeof historyIndex!=='undefined'?historyIndex:-1;
      // Once the restore's DOM/layout work has settled, refresh only the current history
      // head. The redo entries after targetIndex are intentionally left untouched.
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>setTimeout(()=>{
        try{
          if(typeof historyStack==='undefined'||targetIndex<0||historyIndex!==targetIndex)return;
          const fresh=window.snapshotState();
          historyStack[targetIndex]=fresh;
          if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
        }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle restored history refresh:',err);}
      },300)));
      return result;
    };
    wrappedApply.__ethvelleV140Rehydrate=true;window.applySnapshot=wrappedApply;
  }

  const oldUpdateButtons=window.updateHistoryButtons;
  window.updateHistoryButtons=function(){
    const undoBtn=document.getElementById('undoBtn'),redoBtn=document.getElementById('redoBtn');
    const canUndo=typeof historyIndex!=='undefined'&&historyIndex>0;
    const canRedo=typeof historyIndex!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(historyStack)&&historyIndex<historyStack.length-1;
    if(undoBtn){undoBtn.classList.toggle('history-available',canUndo);undoBtn.style.opacity=canUndo?'1':'0.4';undoBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled',canUndo?'false':'true');}
    if(redoBtn){redoBtn.classList.toggle('history-available',canRedo);redoBtn.style.opacity=canRedo?'1':'0.4';redoBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled',canRedo?'false':'true');}
    if(typeof oldUpdateButtons==='function'){
      // The original function only mirrors the same cursor state; call it for any future
      // small UI additions, then re-assert the exact history-index state above.
      try{oldUpdateButtons.apply(this,arguments);}catch(_e){}
      if(undoBtn){undoBtn.style.opacity=canUndo?'1':'0.4';undoBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled',canUndo?'false':'true');}
      if(redoBtn){redoBtn.style.opacity=canRedo?'1':'0.4';redoBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled',canRedo?'false':'true');}
    }
  };

  function v140ResetPendingHistory(){
    try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleV139ResetHistoryTransaction==='function')window.__ethvelleV139ResetHistoryTransaction();}catch(_e){}
  }
  const oldUndoHistory=window.undoHistory;
  if(typeof oldUndoHistory==='function'&&!oldUndoHistory.__ethvelleV140Nav){
    const wrappedUndo=function(){
      const result=oldUndoHistory.apply(this,arguments);
      V140_LAST_HISTORY_NAV_AT=Date.now();v140ResetPendingHistory();
      if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedUndo.__ethvelleV140Nav=true;window.undoHistory=wrappedUndo;
  }
  const oldRedoHistory=window.redoHistory;
  if(typeof oldRedoHistory==='function'&&!oldRedoHistory.__ethvelleV140Nav){
    const wrappedRedo=function(){
      const result=oldRedoHistory.apply(this,arguments);
      V140_LAST_HISTORY_NAV_AT=Date.now();v140ResetPendingHistory();
      if(typeof updateHistoryButtons==='function')updateHistoryButtons();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRedo.__ethvelleV140Nav=true;window.redoHistory=wrappedRedo;
  }

  const oldPersist=window.persistProjectState;
  if(typeof oldPersist==='function'&&!oldPersist.__ethvelleV140History){
    const wrappedPersist=function(opts){
      const result=oldPersist.apply(this,arguments);
      if(opts&&opts.skipHistory===true&&(restoringHistory||Date.now()-V140_LAST_HISTORY_NAV_AT<600))v140ResetPendingHistory();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedPersist.__ethvelleV140History=true;window.persistProjectState=wrappedPersist;
  }

  function v140IsClipGestureTarget(target){
    return !!(target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id],.track-clip[data-bg-id],.anim-clip,.char-clip,.kf-marker,.anim-clip-handle,[data-kf-idx],[data-object-kf-idx],#overlayMediaLayer .overlay-text-render,#canvasStage'));
  }
  function v140IsNavigationTarget(target){
    if(!target||!target.closest)return false;
    if(target.closest('#undoBtn,#redoBtn,#playBtn,#playIcon,#transportZoomBtn,.playhead-wrap,.playhead,#timelineRuler,.timeline-ruler,#timelineFrameReadout,.timeline-frame-readout,#evcaZoomPlay,#evcaZoomOut,#evcaZoomScrub,.evca-zoom-scrub-wrap,.evca-zoom-scrub-track'))return true;
    const scroller=target.closest('#timelineScroll');
    if(scroller&&!v140IsClipGestureTarget(target))return true;
    return false;
  }
  const pointerStarts=new Map();
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(!e||!e.target)return;
    if(v140IsNavigationTarget(e.target)){v140ResetPendingHistory();return;}
    if(v140IsClipGestureTarget(e.target))pointerStarts.set(e.pointerId,{x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,moved:false});
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    const state=pointerStarts.get(e.pointerId);if(!state)return;
    if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-state.x,e.clientY-state.y)>5)state.moved=true;
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
    const state=pointerStarts.get(e.pointerId);if(!state)return;
    pointerStarts.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(!state.moved){v140ResetPendingHistory();V140_LAST_POINTER_DRAG=false;}
    else V140_LAST_POINTER_DRAG=true;
    setTimeout(()=>{V140_LAST_POINTER_DRAG=false;},260);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{if(pointerStarts.has(e.pointerId)){pointerStarts.delete(e.pointerId);v140ResetPendingHistory();}},true);
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(!e||!e.target)return;
    if(v140IsNavigationTarget(e.target)||v140IsClipGestureTarget(e.target)&&!V140_LAST_POINTER_DRAG)v140ResetPendingHistory();
